**Chapter 66 — ZS-A17 v1.5: Macro-Holonomy and Spin-Structure Selection — The Curvature–Spin–Metric Trichotomy, the Spin–Metric Independence No-Go, and the Honest Status of O-Q16.12**

ZS-A17 v1.5 \[Astrophysics / Macro-Holonomy theme, 32/32 PASS (16 corpus-consistency \+ 16 executed, Appendix B), NEW v9.0\] settles, rather than overclaims, the status of the corpus's single bedrock obstruction Z \= ∂X (O-Q16.12) by drawing the macro-holonomy of X into a clean trichotomy and proving that its last leg is a no-go. **Theorem A** fixes the curvature of X by the geometric impedance **A** \= 35/437. **Theorem B / Corollary 5.2** shows the spinor lift is the deck ℤ₂ of Spin(3) → SO(3), forced by topology and NOT a function of **A** (curvature does not give spin). **Theorem E** (DERIVED, via the PROVEN theorems of Borchers and Wiesbrock) reconstructs the boundary co-orientation, at the level of the abstract information line, from the X→Y record-flow modular data. The one remaining question — whether the 3D spatial metric of X, too, follows from information — is answered **no** by **Theorem F (Spin–Metric Independence, NO-GO)** on two independent grounds. *Spectrally*: by Connes's reconstruction theorem the metric dimension of a spectral triple is the Weyl exponent of its Dirac spectrum (λ\_n ∼ n^{1/p}); reconstructing the 3-manifold X requires p \= 3, but the corpus's candidate operators are point-like — the i-tetration transfer operator has spectral zeta finite for all s \> 0 (metric dimension **p \= 0**, since |μ| \= √0.7948 \< 1), the Kraus half-angle is 2×2 (p \= 0), and a single record-flow half-sided modular inclusion yields the 1-dimensional affine group A(1) — so NONE has the n^{1/3} Weyl growth a 3D metric requires (executed, B.10–B.13). *Structurally*: the ZS-F18 finite/infinite Möbius polarity forbids a finite X-bulk from carrying an infinite p \= 3 metric — the Z-bottleneck IS the no-go. Hence the 3D metric is an independent datum, and the "dim(X) \= 3 emergence" the corpus does prove (ZS-S15 Theorem S15.2 / ZS-F18 §6.4, the commutator \[J\_{R₁}, J\_{R₂}\] \= J\_S) delivers the three **SO(3) rotation-algebra generators** — the finite rotation-algebra 3 — not the Riemannian metric 3; conflating the two is the Theorem-F category error. v1.5 strengthens both halves of the verdict without changing it: the metric no-go is now structural as well as spectral, and the co-orientation *hosting* of the record algebra is upgraded from OPEN to **DERIVED-CONDITIONAL** (the multi-cell ITPFI ⊗\_v A\_ZS,v of the single-cell algebra A\_ZS \= M₃ ⊕ ℂ ⊕ M₅ on a non-tracial state is Type III by Takesaki, conditional on the ZS-M17 continuum reconstruction), while the *inclusion source* — an actual one-sided modular inclusion with Takesaki resolution — stays **HYPOTHESIS-strong** (unsupplied). Net: the metric half of O-Q16.12 is an essential NO-GO, its co-orientation half improved but not fully closed; **O-Q16.12 remains NOT closed**, and the corpus's own single-postulate structure forbids overclaiming otherwise. The single external advance that would change the verdict is named: a proof of an emergent metric supplying both a metric-dimension-3 Dirac operator and a genuine one-sided inclusion. Dependencies: ZS-F2 (A), ZS-M3 (dim Z \= 2, j \= 1/2, the 4π closure), ZS-F18 (finite/infinite polarity), ZS-S15 (\[J\_{R₁}, J\_{R₂}\] \= J\_S), ZS-Q11 (A\_ZS \= M₃ ⊕ ℂ ⊕ M₅), ZS-F23 (ITPFI / Type III), ZS-F19 (ΔK\_Ω \= −ln 2 modular ratio), ZS-Q12V (Z \= ∂X), ZS-M17 (continuum reconstruction). → ZS-A17 v1.5 \[32/32 PASS\]; the curvature–spin–metric trichotomy, the Spin–Metric Independence No-Go (the 3D metric is point-like-irreducible, not reconstructible from A or spin), the co-orientation hosting upgraded to Type III DERIVED-CONDITIONAL, and the honest verdict that O-Q16.12 is not closed.

**Chapter 67 — ZS-F25 v2.0: The Pulsation Holonomy is Abelian by Foundation — The Place-Selection Theorem, the Detector–Locator Classification, and the Reduction of Weil Positivity to a Single Inductive Rung**

ZS-F25 v2.0 \[Foundations / RH-Bridge theme, 44/44 PASS, math-spine-pure (A, Q, z\* not consumed by any proof), NEW v9.0\] asks whether the self-referential i-tetration dynamics on the 2D Z-sector boundary can, by itself, reproduce the Riemann/Selberg–Maass spectrum — the "locator" of the zeros — and answers, on multiple independent grounds, that it cannot, sharpening the ZS-F24 honest terminus to a single residual whose totality is RH. The holonomy-theoretic core (v1.0): the pulsation's Wilczek–Zee holonomy is **abelian (U(1)×U(1))**, forced by the corpus's single global complex structure (dim Z \= 2 \= ℂ, the anti-quaternion uniqueness of ZS-F5), so the pulsation can twist the Selberg trace formula only abelianly. The **Place-Selection Theorem** (§5, DERIVED) promotes the v1.0 archimedean/adelic \= abelian/non-abelian unification from HYPOTHESIS-strong to a theorem: three classical PROVEN results — Pontryagin (connected locally compact division rings are ℝ, ℂ, ℍ), Ostrowski (complete archimedean fields are ℝ or ℂ), and the triviality of continuous homomorphisms from connected to totally disconnected groups — combine with three corpus-locked inputs (dim Z \= 2; the anti-quaternion exclusion of ℍ; the continuous faithful U(1)\_Z of ZS-F1) to force the Z-sector substrate to be **ℂ, the unique archimedean completion, and to exclude every p-adic substrate**; the pulsation lives at the archimedean place as a theorem, not an analogy. The paper's principal external export (§6–§7) is the **Detector–Locator Classification**: a four-invariant necessary-condition test — holonomy class, Kolmogorov–Sinai entropy, place support, and spectral sign — for any dynamical or operator-theoretic proposal aimed at zeta spectra, each necessity anchored in a PROVEN classical theorem (three-gap/Steinhaus; Huber–Margulis geodesic counting and the Selberg–Weyl law; Kronecker–Weber; Connes-1999 absorption). Benchmarked against Berry–Keating, Bender–Brody–Müller, Mayer's Gauss-map operator, Bost–Connes, and Connes–Consani–Moscovici, it reproduces exactly what each program is known to deliver and classifies the Z-Spin pulsation as a detector — concordant with ZS-F24 by an independent route. v1.2 makes the place-test failure unconditional: by the Guinand–Weil explicit formula (PROVEN, unconditional) the zero spectrum's Fourier transform is singularly supported exactly on {±k log p} with weights Λ(n)/√n, and a rank lemma (ℚ-linear independence of {log p}) gives rank 1 versus infinite rank, so the sole statement left OPEN is **Weil positivity** — whose equivalence to RH is itself PROVEN. v2.0 closes the blueprint's logical perimeter, absorbing what would have been a successor paper: all proposed positivity formats (kernel, defect-square, compression) are PROVEN-equivalent to Weil positivity (GNS/Kolmogorov); full positivity reduces to an exhaustion of finite prime windows S\_L \= {p^k : k log p ≤ 2L} whose archimedean base rung is PROVEN (Connes–Consani 2021); the induction step is given its exact generalized-Schur form with singular blocks handled (Albert 1969); and corpus-PROVEN no-gos (ADS-5 scalar-kernel exclusion, ZS-M22; M31.0 non-separability, ZS-M31/F21-III) force any realization to be operator-valued and cross-coupled under Z-Spin mediation. After these closures exactly one statement remains open — the **inductive rung — and its totality is RH**. The remaining corpus gate (X-sector \= X₀(11); FM13-6) is unchanged and OPEN. Dependencies: ZS-F24 (i-tetration \= archimedean detector, α\_BK \= −ln|z\*| \= 0.566417), ZS-M4 (boost), ZS-F5 (dim Z \= 2 \= ℂ, anti-quaternion exclusion), ZS-F1 (U(1)\_Z), ZS-M1 (z\*), ZS-M22 (ADS-5), ZS-M31/F21-III (M31.0); imports Pontryagin, Ostrowski, Guinand–Weil, Connes–Consani 2021, Albert 1969\. → ZS-F25 v2.0 \[44/44 PASS\]; the abelian-by-foundation pulsation holonomy, the Place-Selection Theorem forcing the Z-substrate to be the archimedean ℂ (excluding all p-adic places, DERIVED), the four-invariant Detector–Locator export, and the v2.0 reduction of Weil positivity to the single inductive rung whose totality is RH. **NO claim on RH.**

**Chapter 68 — ZS-F26 v1.4: The Operator Ladder and the Unconditional GNS Core — Douglas Uniformization of the Weil-Positivity Rung, High-Frequency Coercivity, and Gate D5 as an Isometric Colimit**

ZS-F26 v1.4 \[Foundations / RH-Bridge theme, 47/47 PASS, math-spine-pure, NEW v9.0\] closes the three audit items ZS-F25 v2.0 left at the boundary of Gate D5 and registers exactly what cannot be closed. (i) **Unconditional GNS core.** The Weil form restricted to any window of autocorrelation reach below log 2 is positive (Connes–Consani 2021, IMPORTED-PROVEN), so its GNS/Kolmogorov quotient H\_base exists unconditionally (Theorem 4.1), all window cores of a fixed length are canonically isometric under the scaling flow (Proposition 4.2), and minimal-Kolmogorov uniqueness identifies H\_base with the closed span of the Connes–Consani Sonin-compression defect vectors (Theorem 4.3). (ii) **Unconditional infinite-dimensionality.** A high-frequency coercivity theorem (Theorem 4.4) proves dim H\_base \= ∞ with explicit derived constants: Re ψ(1/4 \+ ir/2) is strictly increasing in |r| from its global minimum ψ(1/4) \= −γ − π/2 − 3 log 2 \= −4.227454 (Lemma 4.6), a Plancherel-normalized leakage lemma bounds the low-frequency mass fraction by 8Rℓ / (π³(1−δ\_K)²K) (Lemma 4.5′), and the admissible witness **(R₀, K₀) \= (17.082, 24\)** certifies Q\_W \> 0 on an infinite-dimensional tail subspace (positivity margin \+0.125, constants c\_leak \= 20.38, c\_pole \= 0.631 in closed form) — converting the second audit item from a registration into a theorem. (iii) **Douglas uniformization of the rung.** The Section-Certification Gap theorem (Theorem 6.1, PROVEN) shows every finite section of a rung block can be positively completed (section Schur bound H\_N \= Σ\_{n≤N} 1/n) while no positive completion of the full block exists for any finite diagonal datum — rung certification is intrinsically a uniform, infinite-dimensional statement. The Rung Criterion (Lemma 6.2 \= Lemma 11.4′) restates the rung without pseudoinverses; its bounded-realization shadow is the Douglas–Shmul'yan contraction criterion B \= A^{1/2}KC^{1/2} with ‖K‖ ≤ 1 (Theorem 6.3, IMPORTED), so the Z-Spin mediation of every rung is contractive (Corollary 6.5) and, with F25 Theorem 11.6 and Lemma M31.0, operator-valued, cross-coupled, and Π\_Z-sandwiched (Theorem 6.6) — a refinement, not a correction, of F25 Lemma 11.4. (iv) **D5 as a colimit.** The window GNS cores form a directed system whose connecting maps are isometries exactly where positivity holds; RH ⇔ the system is total ⇔ the isometric colimit H\_∞ exists, on which the scaling flow is a strongly continuous unitary group whose self-adjoint generator has spectrum equal to the zero set, simple on the cyclic minimal subrealization (Theorems 5.2–5.3). (v) **Rung 1 exactly.** The Exact-Half Lemma (Lemma 7.2, PROVEN: ‖Re T\_τ‖ \= 1/2 exactly on every window shorter than 2 log 2\) gives the sharp rung-1 perturbation bound, and the Master Inequality **MI(2)** is formulated as the exact content of rung 1; its simplest sufficient route is closed by preregistered numerics (two-sided explicit-formula agreement at 0.049% and 0.0030% over 160 zeros) that expose the archimedean block as indefinite on every rung window while the full Weil form stays positive — rung positivity is produced by **arithmetic–archimedean cross-cancellation, not domination**, in agreement with F25 Theorem 11.6; MI(2)'s truth is OPEN and is not claimed. (vi) **Sector shadow** \[OBSERVATION\]: the first two rung bases are 2 and 3 — the unique prime pair solving (p−1)(q−1) \= p (ZS-M19 T1) — and the first composite control is 6 \= 2·3, so the corpus sector triple (Z, X, Y) \= (2, 3, 6\) coincides with (rung₁, rung₂, control₁); and the unconditional positivity reach log 2 equals the Z-bottleneck channel capacity ln 2 (ZS-F8 / ZS-Q7) — both exact, parameter-free, and used in no proof. (vii–x) v1.2–v1.4 add the Connes-window bridge experiment (at the prime-13 window the minimal eigenvector of Q\_W has exactly six near-zeros on \[0, 40\], and they are the six zero ordinates γ₁–γ₆), its variational mechanism the simultaneous notch bound |ĝ\_min(γ\_k)| ≤ √(λ\_min/2) (Proposition 7.10), and the executed ℓ-scan establishing the first-order Newton-step offset–margin law (Proposition 7.11; 34 (window, zero) pairs across five decades of λ\_min, measured/predicted ratio median 1.04), with the resolution count growing monotonically 3 → 6 as the positivity margin collapses; the same windowed object is minimized in Connes's February 2026 survey to approximate the zeros, the two readings sharing the finite → infinite Euler-product open step O-F26.5. **Chapter 69 — ZS-F27 v1.3: The Register Fourier–Langlands Structure — The Seam Factorization J \= F²S, the Depth-One Obstruction, and the Three-Component Decomposition of the Adelic Locator**

ZS-F27 v1.3 \[Foundations / RH-Bridge theme, 42/42 PASS (script v1.3; the v1.0 27/28-vs-stated-28/28 script incident is documented in §10 as a correction of record), math-spine-pure, NEW v9.0\] adds one internal and one external layer to the ZS-F24 → ZS-F26 terminus, both at zero free parameters. **Internally**, the corpus register ℂ¹¹ is shown to carry an exact, previously unregistered Fourier–Langlands structure. The discrete Fourier operator F on ℂ¹¹ satisfies the **Seam Factorization J \= F²S \= S⁻¹F²** (Theorem 3.3, PROVEN): the corpus seam involution J is the parity-square of F composed with the unit shift, and J is the unique parity-translate inverting the register position operator D \= diag(j − 5\) (Theorem 3.4, the Canonical Seam–Fourier Theorem, for every odd N). The corpus transfer family W\_p \= diag(e^{2πi(j−5)/p}) is the rational-time sampling **W\_p \= U(1/p)** of the single flow U(t) \= e^{2πitD}, with order(W\_p) \= p exactly; its multiplicative closure is ⨁\_p ℤ/p with Pontryagin dual ∏\_p 𝔽\_p (Theorem 4.2, PROVEN). This yields the **Depth-One Obstruction** (Theorem 5.1, DERIVED): the register Wilson algebra realizes every prime at arithmetic depth exactly one, while the locator target requires the full prime-power towers {k log p} with ζ\_p(k) weights (ZS-F25 Theorems 6.4-N/6.4-W) — the deficit is precisely the k ≥ 2 rungs of the ZS-F26 window ladder, a second no-go complementing the ZS-F25 rank obstruction. The register trace formula Tr U(t) \= sin(11πt)/sin(πt) (the order-11 Dirichlet kernel, PROVEN) has finite integer Fourier support and zero arithmetic singular support, confirming **detector status at trace-formula level**. The Fourier conjugate family W\_p^∨ \= FW\_pF⁻¹ is circulant; (W\_p, W\_p^∨) is a non-commuting Weyl-type clock–shift pair, and F intertwines the diagonal (Wilson) and circulant (convolution) algebras — the zero-dimensional instance of GL(1) geometric Langlands duality. Combining J \= F²S with the ZS-M25 PROVEN identity L\_{1−s} \= J·L\_s†·J exhibits the corpus functional-equation involution as the **square of the register Fourier operator**, a finite-dimensional shadow of the Tate mechanism (DERIVED-interpretation); a negative result records that J is a charge-conjugation involution (JW\_pJ \= W\_p\*), not an S-duality — the duality operator of the register is F, not J. v1.1 adds the DFT spectral-multiset uniqueness (3, 3, 3, 2\) at N \= 11 and at no other N (Proposition 3.7, the dim Z link at OBSERVATION) and the exact freeness of the logarithmic prime sampling (Lemma 5.4, via unique factorization and Lindemann). v1.2 opens the seam-constrained finite-Fourier programme with theorems stated and proved Z-Spin-free: the Seam-Odd Uncertainty Theorem on prime cyclic groups (for nonzero seam-odd f, |supp f|·|supp f̂| ≥ 2(p − 1), a doubling of the prime Donoho–Stark bound; Theorem 3.9, via Tao), the Finite Trace Rigidity Theorem (no finite-dimensional self-adjoint flow under any time sampling can produce a Guinand–Weil logarithmic singular support; Theorem 5.5), the half-angle phase pinning of seam-invariant masks (Lemma 3.11, seeding the ZS-F28 programme), and the generation identity S \= F²J (Proposition 6.6). v1.3 adds the pilot closure of that programme: on ℤ\_{pⁿq} the seam-invariant Fuglede equivalence is the restriction of the imported Malikiosis–Kolountzakis theorem (Corollary 3.12, DERIVED, explicitly not a new theorem), seam-invariance is stable under every quotient (Lemma 3.13), non-vacuity is established by exhaustive enumeration at N \= 15 (255 non-empty seam-invariant subsets, 16 spectral \= 16 tiling), and the ZS-F28 main target (N \= pⁿqᵐ seam-invariant spectral ⇒ tile) is formally designated with a five-step skeleton whose open core is a Lam–Leung-type Key Lemma. **Externally**, the paper imports the 2024 closure of the unramified geometric Langlands conjecture (Gaitsgory–Raskin et al., GLC I–V), V. Lafforgue's function-field parameterization, the Fargues–Scholze geometrization of local Langlands, and the Etingof–Frenkel–Kazhdan analytic Langlands results, and proves the **Three-Component Decomposition** (Theorem 8.2, DERIVED-CONDITIONAL under the explicitly stated Langlands-reading Condition L): the adelic-locator OPEN decomposes as (A) the global curve over ℚ — OPEN at the frontier; (B) the non-abelian spectral correspondence — PROVEN wherever a curve exists, closed externally in 2024; (C) the unitarization layer — partial (EFK proven cases). The locator OPEN is thereby relocated and compressed, not closed; component (B), closed externally in 2024 but never registered by the ZS-F24 → ZS-F26 arc, enters the corpus frame here as a theorem of the external frontier, and gate FM13-5 is reframed exactly as the gap between the harmonic embedding p ↦ 1/p (torsion, register side) and the logarithmic embedding p ↦ log p (free, locator side) of the primes into one flow. The genre is stated explicitly: an operator-normalization and registration paper whose mathematical constituents are standard finite Fourier analysis; the principal contribution is corpus-internal (the identification of the LOCKED operators J and W\_p as projections of one canonical Fourier package, so their definitions are forced, not chosen). Dependencies: the ZS-F24 → ZS-F25 → ZS-F26 arc, ZS-M25 (L\_{1−s} \= J·L\_s†·J), ZS-F25 Theorems 6.4-N/6.4-W, the ZS-F26 window ladder; imports GLC I–V / Lafforgue / Fargues–Scholze / EFK / Tao / Malikiosis–Kolountzakis / Lindemann. → ZS-F27 v1.3 \[42/42 PASS\]; the register Fourier–Langlands structure — J \= F²S, the Depth-One Obstruction (the register realizes every prime at depth one, missing the k ≥ 2 towers; DERIVED), the order-11 Dirichlet-kernel detector trace, the functional-equation involution as the Fourier square (finite Tate shadow), and the Three-Component Decomposition relocating the adelic-locator OPEN into (A) frontier curve / (B) 2024-closed non-abelian Langlands / (C) partial EFK unitarization; seeds ZS-F28. **NO claim on RH.**

**Chapter 70 — ZS-F28 v1.0: Seam-Constrained Finite Fourier Analysis II — The Symmetrized Lam–Leung Theorem and the Honest Death of the Single-Direction Key Lemma**

ZS-F28 v1.0 \[Math Spine / Seam-Constrained Programme theme, 17/17 PASS (zs\_f28\_verify\_v1\_0.py, exact integer cyclotomic arithmetic, seed 20260612), NEW v9.0\] proves the open core of the seam-restricted Fuglede programme registered in ZS-F27 §3.7. Let d \= pᵃqᵇ with p ≠ q primes, and let the seam be the involution x ↦ −1−x of ℤ\_d. The **Symmetrized Lam–Leung Theorem** (Theorem 4.3, PROVEN) states: every seam-invariant ℕ-multiset of ℤ\_d that admits a decomposition into p-cycles and q-cycles admits a seam-symmetric such decomposition. The proof is constructive and short — reflect any decomposition across a set of orbit representatives of the induced seam on the class lattice ℤ\_{p^{a−1}} × ℤ\_{q^{b−1}}; the gluing identity is precisely the seam-invariance of the multiset, and the fixed classes are handled by the defect identities of Lemma 4.1 (the symmetry defect of any decomposition is a class function, antisymmetric under the seam, hence zero on fixed classes over ℤ). An alternative gauge-theoretic proof reduces positivity to two interval identities, U∘σ \+ δ \= U and L∘σ \+ δ \= L, verified exhaustively. Combined with the imported de Bruijn–Lam–Leung structure theorem, every seam-invariant vanishing ℕ-multiset of d-th roots of unity has a seam-symmetric cycle decomposition (Corollary 4.5); in the squarefree case a \= b \= 1 the statement degenerates to its strongest form — every decomposition of a seam-invariant multiset is automatically symmetric (Corollary 4.4). A **negative result fixes the programme's shape**: the stronger single-direction collapse conjectured at the programme's registration (the ZS-F27 §3.7 Step-3 Key Lemma) is FALSE — of the 379 seam-invariant vanishing subsets of ℤ₄₅, exactly 96 are genuinely mixed, requiring both cycle types in every decomposition (§5, exhaustive); the conjectured form is retired and replaced. The literature position is fixed by external search (June 2026): unrestricted Fuglede on ℤ\_{pᵐqⁿ} is known only when an exponent is at most 6 or p^{m−2} \< q⁴ (Malikiosis 2022), while Tile ⇒ Spectral is known for all m, n (Łaba 2002); the programme's main target — seam-invariant spectral ⇒ tile for all m, n — therefore aims exactly at the open region, with the seam symmetry as the proposed replacement for the exponent and size conditions. With Theorem 4.3 the skeleton's Step 3 is closed; the open core moves to the global induction (Steps 4–5), registered **O-F28.1**. The reconnaissance protocol that ZS-F27 v1.3 registered did exactly what it was designed to do — it killed the conjectured form of the Key Lemma before a paper was built on it and surfaced the form that is actually true. All results of §3–§5 are stated and proved without Z-Spin vocabulary; the corpus interface is confined to §9. Dependencies: ZS-F27 v1.3 §3.5–§3.7 (the programme registration, Lemma 3.11 half-angle pinning, Lemma 3.13 quotient stability, Corollary 3.12 base family); imports de Bruijn–Lam–Leung, Malikiosis 2022, Łaba 2002, Coven–Meyerowitz. (Note: this Light edition registers the source body at v1.0; the working title carried in some indices, with the additional "Equivariant Extension" and "Purity Frontier" subtitle, anticipates a later version — the v1.0 source proves the Symmetrized Lam–Leung Theorem and the ℤ₄₅ falsification, and registers the global induction as O-F28.1.) → ZS-F28 v1.0 \[17/17 PASS\]; the Symmetrized Lam–Leung Theorem (a seam-invariant cycle decomposition can always be made seam-symmetric by reflection across orbit representatives, PROVEN, two proofs), the squarefree rigidity (Corollary 4.4), the death of the single-direction Key Lemma at ℤ₄₅ (96 of 379 genuinely mixed), and the open core relocated to the global induction O-F28.1; stated Z-Spin-free, corpus interface §9.

**Chapter 71 — ZS-F29 v1.2: Prime-Blind Lifts Cannot Generate Prime-Power Depth — The Socle Obstruction, the Koenigs–Abel Height Coordinate, and the Local Height Model**

ZS-F29 v1.2 \[Foundations / RH-Bridge theme, 30/30 PASS (zs\_f29\_verify\_v1\_2.py, mpmath 120 digits \+ exact rational arithmetic, seed 20260612), math-spine-pure (A not consumed numerically), the terminal addendum to the closing RH-bridge release, NEW v9.0\] proves that a one-parameter unitary flow sampled at the prime reciprocals {1/p} cannot be promoted to prime-power depth by any prime-independent auxiliary structure, and supplies the constructive counterpart by honest import. The **negative layer** has two parts. The **Socle Obstruction** (Theorem 4.6, PROVEN, stated for arbitrary groups): the subgroup of ℚ/ℤ generated by {1/p : p prime} is exactly the socle ⊕\_p ℤ/p — the elements of squarefree order — so for any group G and any homomorphism π : G → ℚ/ℤ whose generators all map into the socle, the image of π lies in the socle, and no element maps to 1/p^k for k ≥ 2 (the Squarefree Span Lemma 4.1; operator instance Theorem 4.3 on ℂ¹¹ ⊗ K). A spectral corollary excludes conjugation routes (Corollary 4.4′, with a documented correction of record: the v1.0 proof step "the eigenvalue multiset determines t up to sign" is false — counterexample spec U(1/11) \= spec U(2/11), check G-21 — while the conclusion stands via the conjugation-invariant maximal order, check G-22), and the Universal-Multiplier Mismatch (Corollary 4.5) excludes rate-matching by the single Koenigs multiplier. The **positive layer** has two parts. **Chapter 72 — ZS-A18 v1.5: A Recombination No-Go for Z-Spin Goldstone Dark Matter — The Massless Dichotomy, the Sound-Speed Window, and the Time-Dependent-Mass Obstruction (v1.0 Closure Retracted)**

ZS-A18 v1.5 \[Astrophysics theme, 25/25 Internal Consistency Audit PASS, zero new fitted Z-Spin parameters (external h, N\_eff, T\_CMB used), NEW v9.0\] is a viability test — not a closure — of the identification of cold dark matter with the gradient energy of the massless Goldstone phase θ of the broken U(1)\_Z, against the height of the third CMB acoustic peak, and it returns a NO-GO that retracts the over-strong "closure" claim of v1.0. The third peak requires a clustering, pressureless component before recombination with equation of state w ≈ 0, sound speed c\_s² ≈ 0, adiabatic initial conditions, and passive (coherent) evolution. Two results establish the obstruction. **(1) The Recombination Equation-of-State Dichotomy.** An *exactly massless* Goldstone has w \= 1 (kination, c\_s \= 1\) when time-dependent and w̄ \= −1/3 (anisotropic global monopole) when a static halo — **never the w ≈ 0** the third peak demands. This is the corpus's own ZS-M43 result, and it *contradicts*, rather than supports, the v1.0 gates A18.3 (w \= 0\) and A18.4 (c\_s² ≈ 0). **(2) The Isocurvature Gate, computed.** Correcting the backwards "frozen → suppressed" logic of v1.0, a spectator Goldstone broken before or during inflation carries fluctuations δθ \= H\*/(2πf\_θ); using the Z-Spin tensor-to-scalar ratio r \= 0.0089 (which fixes H\* ≈ 2.3×10¹³ GeV) and the Planck-scale decay constant f\_θ ∼ M\_P, the CDM isocurvature fraction is β\_iso ≈ 4.4×10⁻³, below the Planck 2018 limit β\_iso \< 0.038 — the suppression due to the large (Planck-scale) f\_θ, not to "freezing". The headline obstruction is the **Time-Dependent-Mass conservation law**: no single Goldstone mass m\_θ — constant OR time-dependent — both preserves the ε-Halo and clusters at the third peak. A decreasing mass (m↓ as the universe cools) dilutes the field as ρ ∝ m·a⁻³, so bridging the ×9.8 recombination-clustering mass gap costs an identical ×9.8 background-density gap: **the obstruction is conserved**. The four would-be gates collapse to a single Goldstone-mass question, registered **OPEN (G-A18.M)**; F-A18.2 is a cold-fluid control run, and G-A18.M is OPEN only for non-minimal / non-adiabatic constructions. The honest status is **HYPOTHESIS-strong, a four-gate closure programme**, with the v1.0 closure RETRACTED and the verification banner changed to internal-consistency-audit PASS with 0/4 core dynamical gates numerically closed. Dependencies: ZS-M43 (the massless equation-of-state dichotomy w ∈ {1, −1/3}), ZS-A16 (CDM \= massless U(1)\_Z Goldstone gradient; the §11.4 frozen/scaling-correlator statement corrected to a literature link to Pen–Seljak–Turok 1997, not a theorem), ZS-U1 (r \= 0.0089), ZS-F1 (broken U(1)\_Z), ZS-F2 (geometric CDM). → ZS-A18 v1.5 \[25/25 Internal Consistency Audit PASS\]; the Recombination No-Go — a massless Goldstone is w ∈ {1, −1/3} and never the w ≈ 0 of the third peak (ZS-M43), the isocurvature fraction β\_iso ≈ 4.4×10⁻³ safely below Planck, and the conserved time-dependent-mass obstruction — collapsing the four gates to the single OPEN Goldstone-mass question G-A18.M, with the v1.0 closure retracted (the carrier No-Go that ZS-A19 answers by changing ontology).

ZS-A19 v3.1 \[Astrophysics theme, 22/22 independent recomputations PASS (polyhedral, graph-spectral, cross-transfer, symplectic-branching, and the C1/C2 substrate computations; Appendices C–H), zero new fitted parameters, NEW v9.0\] answers the ZS-A18 NO-GO not by contesting it but by changing the carrier: it abandons the premise that dark matter is a collection of objects (Planck-mass vortex cores, vortons, primordial-black-hole relics) — the premise responsible for the over-closure, \~10⁹ GeV formation-scale, and current-carrier obstructions, all artifacts of the particle ontology — and instead implements ZS-F2's definition of CDM as a geometric boundary tension through a conserved pullback / Brown–Kuchař dust (w \= 0, c\_s² \= 0, ρ ∝ a⁻³) that leaves the Goldstone exactly massless. ℤ) \= ℤ^r ⊕ T and m \= b₁(Γ), Künneth and Poincaré–Lefschetz give H³((M\_Z, ∂M\_Z) × Γ; ℤ) ≅ ℤ ⊕ ℤ^{mr} ⊕ T^{⊕m}; for the A19 one-cross-edge seam graph, b₁(Γ₃₈) \= 59 \+ 7 \= 66, so H³\_seam ≅ ℤ ⊕ ℤ^{66r} ⊕ T^{⊕66} and a **unique parent charge exists iff H₁(M\_Z) \= 0** (the homology-ball condition M0). **(ii) Homology-Ball Selection (Corollary 2\) and a sharper L\_clock.** Single-clock \+ no-torsion ⇔ M0; the unrestricted coupling classification remains OPEN); the I\_h-invariant cold functional is two-dimensional (the 12-pentagon and 20-hexagon orbits), and a \= b is forced by graph connectedness, not by I\_h. **(iv) Polyhedral Graph-Discrimination Theorem (Theorem 4, PROVEN) — the new result.** Three solids realize 32 faces and hence the abundance 32/121 identically — the truncated icosahedron (12 pentagons \+ 20 hexagons), the truncated dodecahedron (20 triangles \+ 12 decagons), and the icosidodecahedron (20 triangles \+ 12 pentagons) — so a ratio-only likelihood is polyhedron-blind. **PART XX — Post-v10.0 Absorption: Thirteen New Source Papers (v11.0)**

**§0. Abstract**

PART XX absorbs the thirteen post-v10.0 source papers (paper 168 and papers 169–180) as Chapters 90–102, advancing coverage from 167 to the full 180-paper corpus. It also settles a numbering item of record: ZS-F35 (paper 168\) was cited only as a planned/forward companion of ZS-F34 in PART XIX and had never received its own Book chapter; PART XX registers it as Chapter 90, so the coverage runs gap-free from 168 to 180\. PART XX is organized as three arcs. The **charge-unit / structural-factor Foundations arc** (ZS-F35 → ZS-F36) isolates the structural dimensionless factor 36**A**/**Q** \= 1260/4807 of the odd-sector vacuum susceptibility and its UV normalization, leaving one dimensionless gate (C\_norm) and one dimensionful scale (M\_UV / M\_eff). The **modular-depth Math-Spine / clock-registry arc** (ZS-M44 → ZS-A31 → ZS-M46 → ZS-M47 → ZS-F37 → ZS-F38 → ZS-A32 → ZS-F39) builds the machinery that would fix the metric-scale hierarchy: the register-trace normalization ρ\_Q \= I\_Q/**Q**; the effective one-parameter reduction M\_eff; the seam-as-unit-translation and its positive-energy standard pair; the Seam Modular-Depth Theorem; the action-level Two-Leg Law; the Register Clock Identity (H-CLK) and the depth M\_K/M̄\_P \= e^{−2π**Q**}; the Planck-pivot extremal ladder with its executed anti-numerology Monte Carlo (p \= 0.50%); and the seam uniformization theorem with the modular length functor. The **B3 honest-terminus Foundations arc** (ZS-F40 → ZS-F41 → ZS-F42) then executes the finite gates and closes the absolute-scale mechanism programme **negatively** (the √2 Refusal, the Osgood–Phillips–Sarnak exclusion, the θ₁ exclusion), supplies the first conditional odd charge-lattice candidate (USp(10)), and delivers a non-transmutation √N₄ frozen-excess hierarchy generator that closes the dimensionless structure of **χ₋** but terminates honestly at the single dimensionful membrane charge e₆. As with every PART since III, all chapter entries are summarisations of source-paper Verification Summaries; no new theorem is introduced by PART XX itself, every absorbed status is preserved verbatim from the source, and the no-deletion rule is maintained.

**§XX.A — Thirteen New Papers (Light-Edition Body Entries)**

**PART XXI — Post-v11.0 Absorption: Twenty-Two New Source Papers (v12.0)**

**§0. Abstract**

PART XXI absorbs the twenty-two post-v11.0 source papers (papers 181–202) as Chapters 103–124, advancing coverage from 180 to the full 202-paper corpus. It is organized as four arcs. The **B3 terminus-and-sequestering Foundations arc** (ZS-F43 → ZS-F44 → ZS-F45 → ZS-F46) re-types the charge-unit residual as a representation-theoretic no-go, proves the parent-factor route to C\_UV closed-negative, audits the null-power route to the same wall, and executes vacuum-energy sequestering on the seam — four independent lines terminating at one calibrated boundary datum (B3-B). The **arithmetic-carrier Math-Spine arc** (ZS-M48 → ZS-M49 → ZS-M50 → ZS-M51 → ZS-M52) transfers Suzuki's finite-interval screw operator from ζ to every real primitive Dirichlet channel, lifts it to the C₂³ quasicrystal carrier with a reusable zero-exclusion certificate, closes the algebraic half of the horizontal cyclotomic clock by an explicit Bost–Connes representation, proves the exact Lambert–Dottie stability threshold s\_c \= e^{sin ρ} with a certified fixed-point census, and settles the arithmetic level of the horizontal clock's measure theory. The **complementarity / measurement arc** (ZS-F47 → ZS-F48 → ZS-Q18 → ZS-M53, with ZS-U12 as the cosmological transfer node) identifies the genuine arithmetic saddle (5, ¼) of the master cocycle, gives its local skew normal form and positive density-matrix intertwiner, replaces the amplitude-damping representative by the **dephasing (QND) representative** and thereby derives the **Born rule** from i-tetration, and closes the mean-channel spectrum exactly at {1, 1, λ, λ̄}. The **cellular Yang–Mills carrier arc** (ZS-S17 → ZS-S18 → ZS-S19 → ZS-S20 → ZS-S21 → ZS-S22 → ZS-S23 → ZS-S24) drives the ZS-S14 master action onto the truncated-icosahedron complex, exhausts and negatively closes every quadratic route to the Hodge measure, replaces selection by construction via the transfer matrix, identifies two universality classes under Goldberg–Coxeter refinement, audits the action-to-Hessian bridge, and finally proves a **finite-carrier spectral-gap theorem with an explicit reflection-positive slab realisation**. As with every PART since III, all chapter entries are summarisations of source-paper Verification Summaries; no new theorem is introduced by PART XXI itself, every absorbed status is preserved verbatim from the source, and the no-deletion rule is maintained.

at a\_TI \= 1 the full-DEC gap is 0.3600376672 (−71.031%), the locked λ₁ is reproduced at a\_TI \= 0.5382277383, and the handover's 1.1947657995 corresponds to an **undeclared implicit** a\_TI \= 0.5489498022 — a single genuine rescaling already sweeps λ₁ over (0, ∞), so **the −3.868% comparison is retracted as a ledger statement**. **Theorem S19.2 (Anchoring Defect Identity):** for every I\_h-invariant diagonal DEC metric and every eigen-channel, dev(λ) \= −\[W₅₆S₅₆(λ) \+ W₆₆S₆₆(λ)\] in closed form, residual below the 10⁻¹² threshold (realized residuals O(10⁻¹⁴) and BLAS-dependent, so the ledger records the threshold, not the run value). **That identity forces a retraction of the paper's own v1.0 claim:** the λ₁-channel anchoring identity is **not** equivalent to pointwise star compatibility — an external reviewer's explicit counterexample satisfies it to 1.6 × 10⁻¹⁵ while violating compatibility, and along its zero locus λ₁ spans −35.3% to \+0.6%; the v1.0 necessity claim is **RETRACTED**. **Theorem S19.6 (Regge-Moduli Exclusion), upgraded to a certified proof:** the circumcentric Regge route is excluded over the **full two-dimensional I\_h-invariant Regge moduli** — the closed-form reduction gives Q − 1 \= P(u)/\[20c(√3u − 1)(u \+ c)\] with P an explicit cubic whose positivity is certified by **outward-rounded interval arithmetic** (P′(2.20) \< 0 \< P′(2.21), P″ ≥ 27.23 \> 0 on the interval, P ≥ 64.80 \> 0), the discriminant argument demoted to corroboration; the v1.3 grid "infimum at t ≈ 2.416" is corrected to the true minimum Q \= 1.1463953345 at t \= 2.4107050485. **Retractions carried:** the ZS-S18 §7 quartic sign (Lemma S19.4); the claim that Λ\_QCD is confined to 261–275 MeV over the whole compatibility line (it diverges as ρ\_F → 0 and tends to 429.7 MeV as ρ\_F → ∞, so the band belongs to the audited window only); the derivation of the counting star from (R) alone, replaced by the explicit, strictly stronger cellular-counting axiom **(R\_C)**; the description of the residual as "finite, enumerated"; and the arithmetic-mean Hamiltonian relation, replaced by the harmonic mean from the Legendre inverse. **Dependency discipline, new in v1.5–v1.6:** the headline results are **UNBUNDLED** — counting-star selection is DERIVED-CONDITIONAL on (R\_C) **alone**, Theorem S19.6 is PROVEN as geometry with its *selection bearing* conditional on (H-UA), and their agreement is recorded as **joint corroboration rather than a shared dependency**; mpmath is promoted from optional import to declared dependency so that 95/95 with seven OPEN is a fixed specification, not an environment-dependent one. **Scope narrowed:** "settles the metric route completely" becomes "settles the **diagonal circumcentric Regge-metric** route completely", and "3-fold everywhere" becomes "threefold at all sampled points of the audited domain ρ\_F ∈ \[10⁻⁴, 10⁶\]", with NC-S19.20 stating that **no no-crossing theorem is proved**. → ZS-S19 v1.6 \[95/95 PASS\]; the anchoring defect identity, the certified Regge-moduli exclusion, the corrected Magnus quartic, and the finite Gauss–Faddeev–Popov programme.

**Chapter 120 — ZS-S20 v2.2: The Cellular Reduction of the Z-Spin Master Action — Projection, Legendre Closure, the Non-Abelian Gauss Law, and the Non-Identifiability of the Truncated-Icosahedron Hodge Measure**

ZS-S20 v2.2 \[Standard Model theme, **212 PASS | 0 FAIL | 22 active OPEN printed and not counted**, composition 183 EXECUTABLE \+ 27 DECLARATIVE \+ 2 PROXY with **no executable check a literal True** (the v1.0 flat "83/83" and the v1.1 literals T14/T92 are both corrected of record), a real static-analysis anti-regression block parsing the suite's own source with Python's ast module, deterministic seed and self-emitted SHA-256, zero free parameters, **NEW v12.0**; the supplied index lists v2.1 — the corpus body is v2.2\] reduces the ZS-S14 SU(3) master action onto the truncated-icosahedron complex K\_TI and asks what fixes the Hodge measure the reduction requires. Successive versions return the same answer with progressively sharper reasons: **nothing in ZS-S14 fixes it**. **The negative results are the substance.** Hermiticity selects nothing, because the adjoint is *defined* by M (Theorem S20.O — **firing pre-registered gate F-S20.8**, the attempt to derive selection from a single Hermitian operator). Every positive-definite matrix is already a Gram matrix, so the metric is free data (S20.N-a). Reading M from the transfer free-energy Hessian returns the M that was put in, exactly and for every positive M (S20.T1). Register democracy fails because **a stationary density is not a kinetic rate**; integrality fails for want of an SU(3) charge-lattice bridge; ρ \= 1 is not symmetry-protected. **One mechanism runs through all of them:** a fixed-point condition built solely from the action admits the action as a solution, and constrains M only if over-determined. Closure therefore needs a non-quadratic or coarse-graining step; the compact-group heat-kernel semigroup is **verified rather than cited** (4.3 × 10⁻¹⁴ for U(1), 2.8 × 10⁻¹⁷ for SU(2)), making the face parameter exactly additive under subdivision and β(A) \= 1/(cA) non-perturbative — which replaces the weight question by an **area-measure** question rather than answering it. **The status is stated in two layers, kept apart.** **LAYER 1 — the arithmetic uniqueness theorem \[PROVEN analytically over all positive reals\]:** within the positive **diagonal** I\_h-invariant family, (H-ALG) integral counting-spectrum degeneracy ∧ (H-TR) the incidence sum rule Tr Δ₂ \= 2E \= 180 have the **unique** solution ρ \= σ \= r \= 1, hence M₁ \= M₂ \= m·I and λ₁ \= 1.2428416164 at multiplicity three — the v2.0 claim from a 729-point rational grid is **withdrawn** in favour of the analytic proof. **LAYER 2 — the physical selection ZS-S14 ⇒ (H-ALG) ∧ (H-TR): NOT PROVEN**; both are HYPOTHESIS-strong postulates, and (H-TR) may be no more than the convention a\_TI \= 1\. **The Yang–Mills bridge is therefore DERIVED-CONDITIONAL, not closed**: the dimensionless λ₁ closes conditionally, Λ\_QCD \= 264.1 MeV does not (F-S19.3 untouched), and the theorem is restricted to the diagonal family (non-diagonal I\_h-equivariant mass matrices and the Whitney/FEEC branch stay open under F-S20.5). **A by-product stands independently:** the characteristic polynomial of B₂B₂ᵀ factors over ℤ as λ(λ−6)⁴(λ−8)⁵(λ²−10λ+22)⁵(λ⁴−22λ³+166λ²−480λ+380)³, the quartic is irreducible over ℚ, and **both ledger eigenvalues λ₁ and λ\_h are its roots** — algebraic integers of degree four with an exact minimal polynomial, where the corpus previously carried ten-digit decimals; that the four roots sum to 22 is Vieta, and that 22 \= 2**Q** reflects register structure is an **observation awaiting a connecting theorem** (v2.0 overstated it). One route was executed and closed negative (restoring the ℓ \= 3, 4 continuum degeneracies splits by 21.3% and 11.5% with mutually inconsistent minima), the anti-numerology Monte Carlo was executed and reported **negative (p \= 33.82%)**, an erratum was filed against ZS-S19 §1.1, and **ten rounds of adversarial review produced ten retractions, each recorded with the statement that killed it** — six sharing one shape, a proxy reported as the target, which is why the companion now carries a PROXY check kind and a basis-label guard. → ZS-S20 v2.2 \[212 PASS\]; the exhaustive negative audit of quadratic-action routes to the Hodge measure, the two-layer arithmetic uniqueness theorem, and the exact minimal polynomial of λ₁ and λ\_h.

**Chapter 121 — ZS-S21 v1.2: The Instrument Construction — Closing the Cellular Transfer-Matrix / Hodge-Measure Sub-Bridge of Z-Spin Yang–Mills**

ZS-S21 v1.2 TERMINAL \[Standard Model theme, **88/88 PASS | 1 PROXY | 24 DECL | 0 FAIL**, zero continuous dimensionless parameters under (H-W) ∧ (Z-A0) ∧ (Z-A1) — two ratios σ and ρ surviving without (Z-A1) — all locked constants unre-fitted, **NEW v12.0**\] carries a **scope declaration placed before its own abstract**, a discipline the Book records as exemplary: in this paper "Yang–Mills bridge" denotes the **cellular transfer-matrix / Hodge-measure sub-bridge only** — the chain group-valued cellular action → transfer matrix → diagonal Hodge measure → dimensionless quadratic spectrum — and **not** closure of non-perturbative SU(3) glueball phenomenology; the exact Wilson quartic, the non-Abelian Gauss–Coulomb–Faddeev–Popov reduction, the 31-mode Lanczos spectrum, the absolute glueball interaction coefficient and continuum scheme-matching all remain outside, and every O(g²) statement of S17–S20 remains DERIVED-PERT-COND at λ\_t ≈ 5.54. *(The title itself is scope-corrected in v1.2 so that the qualifier travels with the citation rather than being lost with a subtitle.)* The paper acts on ZS-S20's **diagnosis** rather than its conclusion: in lattice gauge theory the quadratic Hamiltonian is not a datum to be selected but an **output of the transfer matrix**, constructed from a group-valued action. **Theorem S21.1:** under **(H-W)** — a Wilson-type group-valued plaquette action with compact link variables and nearest-neighbour time coupling — the temporal plaquettes on K\_TI × a\_tℤ are in exact bijection with the 90 edges and the spatial plaquettes with the 32 faces, and no third class of 2-cell exists because dim K\_TI \= 2; hence M₁ and M₂ are diagonal **for every weight assignment**, not merely the uniform one, and gate F-S20.5 — the non-diagonal I\_h-equivariant family that S20 left as its one genuine remaining mathematical risk — is **SUPERSEDED-BY-CONSTRUCTION: there is no family to search**. **Lemma S21.2 \[PROVEN, unconditional\]:** all 90 edges of an Archimedean solid are congruent, so no weight built from a temporal plaquette's own primal geometry can separate the two edge orbits. **Weight uniformity itself is not:** the (5,6) and (6,6) orbits have different ambient stars, so ψ\_e({deg f : f ⊃ e}) and every dual measure do separate them (computed dual ratios 0.8973272361 intrinsic and 0.9105929973 chordal, the latter independently reproducing the ZS-S20 flat-cone value from a rebuilt complex) — so **v1.1's claim that σ \= 1 is unconditional is RETRACTED (S21-R3)**: σ \= 1 is DERIVED-CONDITIONAL, on exactly the same footing as ρ \= 1\. **Reported against interest, twice:** the seed hypothesis that the Wilson action carries a single spatial coupling by construction is **refuted** for non-regular complexes (the Christ–Friedberg–Lee random-lattice action weights each plaquette by its dual/own measure ratio, and A₆/A₅ \= 1.5100902868 on K\_TI); and v1.0's inference that a metric-free carrier alone forces β₅ \= β₆ is **INVALID**, since β\_f \= β\_sψ(n\_f) is metric-free and realises every ρ \> 0 — the ψ(n\_f) obstruction ZS-S19 v1.3 discovered and retracted, recurring one level up (Retraction S21-R2). **The axiom structure:** **(Z-A0)** Metric-Free Carrier removes every dual-measure and area branch; **(Z-A1)** the Orbit-Blind Plaquette Reduction Postulate — expanded in v1.2 to cover **both** anisotropy classes — removes every ambient-combinatorial branch; only together do they give σ \= ρ \= 1 and Δ₂ \= r·B₂B₂ᵀ. (Z-A1) is the ZS-S19 counting axiom (R\_C) restated at group-valued level and extended to the temporal class. **What the transfer matrix contributes is precise and limited: it PROVES diagonality and it PROPAGATES orbit weights; it does not select them**, and §6.3 reports that as a negative result of the construction rather than burying it. That the postulated σ \= 1 returns the independently LOCKED λ₁ is registered as a **non-trivial consistency check and explicitly declined as evidence** for (Z-A1). → ZS-S21 v1.2 \[88/88 PASS\]; the transfer-matrix instrument construction proving diagonality by construction, superseding F-S20.5, and relocating the residual to the two orbit weights and the two axioms that fix them.

**Chapter 122 — ZS-S22 v1.5: The Two Universality Classes of Z-Spin Cellular Yang–Mills — Goldberg–Coxeter Refinement, the Defect-Support Theorem, and the Re-attribution of the Orbit-Blind Postulate**

ZS-S22 v1.5 TERMINAL \[Standard Model theme, **139 verification-ledger entries PASS** — 125 executable checks \+ 13 computed diagnostics \+ 1 proxy, plus 39 declarative — 0 FAIL, zero free parameters, hashed self-contained companion, **NEW v12.0**\] carries an explicit **ledger-wording note** the Book adopts as a reading rule: *"139 PASS" counts ledger entries, not mathematical theorems* — recounted for v1.5 the paper contains **six PROVEN results**, one PROVEN-CONDITIONAL theorem, one PROVEN-CONDITIONAL corollary, two DERIVED-CONDITIONAL theorems and one numerically-supported conjecture, the remainder being executable assertions, diagnostics, one proxy and registry statements; the breakdown sits in the banner **precisely so the count cannot be mistaken for a theorem count**. Its scope declaration is equally explicit: it is a theorem-plus-computation paper about a quadratic Hodge operator on a two-dimensional cell complex, and establishes **no** continuum Yang–Mills measure, **no** SU(3) mass gap and **no** 3+1-dimensional Clay-form statement. It changes the question **from selection to universality** by embedding K\_TI as the first member of the icosahedral Goldberg–Coxeter family **K\_n \= GP(n,n)**, in which the 12 pentagonal curvature defects and their 60 adjacent edges stay finite while the hexagonal bulk grows as 30n². **Lemma S22.0** reduces the computation exactly: for a 2-sphere cell complex B₂B₂ᵀ is the graph Laplacian of the dual 1-skeleton, so Δ\_n(σ, ρ) is a weighted Laplacian on the class-II geodesic triangulation {3,5⁺}\_{n,n} (verified to exact zero at n \= 1, 2, 3). **Theorem S22.1** proves the exact census V\_n \= 60n², E\_n \= 90n², F\_n \= 30n² \+ 2 with exactly 12 pentagons, 30n² − 10 hexagons, 60 (5,6) edges and 90n² − 60 (6,6) edges, verified on every generated n ≤ 12\. **Theorem S22.2 (Defect-Support Theorem)** sharpens the seeded rank target: Δ\_n(σ, ρ) − Δ\_n(1,1) is not merely finite rank but **exactly supported on the closed graph neighbourhood of the defect set**, a principal block of dimension 12 \+ 60 \= **72, independent of n** — with sub-bounds exactly 60 when ρ \= 1 and exactly 24 when σ \= 1; the seeded bound 84 is correct but **not sharp** (it double-counts the 12 pentagon directions), **Retraction S22-R1**, and Corollary S22.2a gives a uniform 72/(30n² \+ 2\) → 0 bound on the spectral-measure deviation. **Theorem S22.3, reported against interest and split by grade:** **(a) PROVEN** — the number of I\_h edge orbits of K\_n is at least 3n²/4 and therefore **diverges**, so the orbit complexity any metric prescription must resolve grows without bound; **(b) COMPUTED over the audited range n ≤ 8** — the circumcentric DEC weights of a shape-regular geodesic realisation acquire 42 distinct edge values by n \= 8 and their bulk deviation from uniformity **grows** to 0.349 rather than decaying, so the *implemented* metric branch escapes the finite-defect class over that range. v1.5 carries the split into the abstract as well as the body: **it is not proved that the DEC weights escape finite-defect support at every n**, and the seed's branch table placement is retracted (S22-R2). The net structural result is the identification of **two — not one — inequivalent refinement limits** of the ZS-S21 instrument, together with the determination of **which of the three ZS-S21 hypotheses actually selects between them** — the re-attribution named in the title. **ZS-S22 does not supersede ZS-S21**, which remains terminal on the finite-K\_TI Hodge-measure selection question. → ZS-S22 v1.5 \[139 ledger entries PASS\]; the exact Goldberg–Coxeter census, the n-independent 72-dimensional Defect-Support Theorem, and the two universality classes with the orbit-blind postulate re-attributed.

**Chapter 123 — ZS-S23 v1.3: The Action-to-Hessian Bridge on the Physical Z-Spin Carrier — the Metric Route, the Clock Route, and the Central-Hessian Theorem**

ZS-S23 v1.3 TERMINAL \[Standard Model theme, **98 executable entries PASS \= 87 theorem-bearing checks (C) \+ 11 COMPUTED diagnostics (X)**, 12 declarative, 0 FAIL, **zero new fitted physical parameters**, hashed companion, **NEW v12.0**\] attacks at action level the two statements on which the whole S-line rests — **(Z-A0)** that the cellular reduction of ZS-S14 is metric-free, and **(Z-A1)** that its orbit weights are blind to the pentagon/hexagon distinction — and **reports a mixed and largely negative result, because that is what the calculations give**. Two ledger disciplines are introduced of record: the **C/X split** (v1.2 recorded all 95 entries as theorem-bearing while its own text demoted eight of them — *a ledger that grades an entry differently from the manuscript is a ledger that cannot be audited*, so the eleven entries whose content is numerical evidence with measured but unbounded discretisation error are now kind **X**, carrying no proof weight); and the replacement of "Zero Free Parameters" by **"Zero New Fitted Physical Parameters"**, since the conformal probe coordinates are search coordinates introduced solely to demonstrate non-identifiability and no physical quantity depends on them. **Step A, metric route — Theorem S23.1:** in the **round** metric the one-parameter I\_h truncation family **never reaches σ \= ρ \= 1**; v1.0's "PROVEN by exhibition" is withdrawn and replaced by an actual proof, with closed forms for r₅(t), A₅, A₆, l₅₆, l₆₆ and the exact t-independent dual arcs, strict monotonicity and root uniqueness following analytically and the strict separation certified at the rational point t \= 7/20 by a genuine **interval-arithmetic enclosure** at widths below 10⁻⁴⁸ — so **gate F-S23.9 is CLOSED-PASS** and no numerical root-find enters the proof. The equal-area point admits the closed form cos r₅ \= cot(π/5)·cot(5π/16). **Step A, conformal correction — Theorem S23.2** provides **strong computed counterevidence** against ZS-S22 v1.2's conclusion that geometry *forces* Class M (v1.2's word "refutes" is itself narrowed, since a COMPUTED application cannot deliver that). Three corrections: **Lemma S23.2a** proves the carrier data v1.0 held fixed are *not* an approximation — the pentagon and hexagon circumcentres are the isolated fixed points of C₅ᵥ and C₃ᵥ, hence metric-independent for every I\_h-invariant metric, and the two dual arcs with the primal (6,6) edge lie in mirror planes and are therefore **stationary geodesics** (wording narrowed from *geodesy*, since totally geodesic does not give global minimality; exactly one of the four arcs, the primal (5,6) edge, is the sole residual approximation, measured at 5.33 × 10⁻³). The reachability claim is re-proved by the **intermediate value theorem** rather than by fsolve, with the abstract statement PROVEN-CONDITIONAL and its application **only COMPUTED** for one hypothesis — v1.1's "each VERIFIED, therefore existence at proof strength" was one grade too strong and is retracted. And the published residual **1.6 × 10⁻¹⁴ is retracted as a solver residual**: re-evaluated at the full-mode reference quadrature the v1.0 root has a true geometric residual of **2.07 × 10⁻³**, eleven orders of magnitude larger — the metric route therefore **still under-determines**, that conclusion strengthening, but its numerical support restated at honest precision. **Step A, clock route — Theorem S23.3** transfers ZS-F38's proved unique-ergodicity result to the carrier under a new named axiom **(Z-A3)**, yielding σ \= ρ \= 1 — and states the objection rather than burying it: **a stationary probability is not a kinetic stiffness** (ZS-S20's unresolved bridge (H-PSM-2)), and ZS-F40's physical clock discharge returned CLOSED-NEGATIVE with a Frobenius deviation of exactly **√2**, unreconciled here. **(Z-A1) is relocated, not eliminated, and the corpus axiom count is unchanged.** **Step B — the Central-Hessian Theorem S23.4:** for compact simple G any C² central Φ has Ad-invariant identity-Hessian, hence by Schur equals κ\_Φ times the Killing form; **Lemma S23.5** proves centrality is *equivalent* to gauge invariance for a single-plaquette term; and **Theorem S23.6** corrects a type error inherited from ZS-S22 v1.2 — the Hessian is a **90 × 90 edge-space** operator and Δ\_{S21} a **32 × 32 face-space** operator, and they are **nonzero-isospectral, not equal**. Single-plaquette locality is exactly (H-W), which remains OPEN, so Step B is **PROVEN-CONDITIONAL on (H-W)**. **The honest one-line summary:** the action-to-Hessian bridge is closed in its **algebraic** half — the Hessian's form is forced, conditionally on (H-W) — and open in its **selection** half, neither route determining the orbit weights, with the class question now OPEN in one specific named place: the **density-versus-rate identification**. → ZS-S23 v1.3 \[98 entries PASS; 87 theorem-bearing\]; the certified metric-route under-determination, the clock route relocated behind (Z-A3), and the Central-Hessian Theorem with its isospectrality correction.

**Chapter 124 — ZS-S24 v1.9: Finite-Carrier Action-to-Gap Closure under the Canonical Holonomy Reduction — The Reflection-Positive Slab Realisation, the Two-Tier Transfer-Matrix Gap, and the Exact Electric-Limit Spectrum of the Z-Spin SU(3) Carrier**

ZS-S24 v1.9 FINAL \[Standard Model theme, **100 ledger entries** — 69 executable checks (36 closed-form, 14 exhaustive, 13 control, 6 confirmation) \+ 16 math-theorem blocks \+ 15 consistency gates — 0 FAIL, zero free parameters, hashed companion, **NEW v12.0**; the supplied index lists v1.4 — the corpus body is v1.9 FINAL\] delivers the positive terminus of the S-line. **The theorem (S24.2).** On a finite cell complex with E edges, G \= SU(3), **M** \= G^E, let **L** be real, symmetric, **uniformly elliptic (E)**, essentially self-adjoint, with **\[L, U(h)\] \= 0** for every h in the local gauge group **(G)**, and let V be gauge-invariant and continuous. Then H\_g \= g²L \+ g⁻²V has **compact resolvent, a positivity-improving heat semigroup, a unique strictly positive — hence gauge-invariant — ground state, and a strictly positive physical gap for every g \> 0**. Theorem S24.2′ builds such an L from a coefficient tensor by Friedrichs extension, settling operator ordering for U-dependent coefficients. **The admissible class is characterised honestly:** (E) and (G) are logically **independent in both directions** (negative controls exhibited), Proposition S24.2d gives the necessary and sufficient condition for (G) — Ad-equivariance of the coefficient tensor — and the three certificates K1–K3 are **sufficient certificates, never a classification**. **The reduction.** §4 declares **(R1)** link variables are the edge holonomies U\_e \= P exp ∫\_e A, and **(R2)** the discrete action is a real, continuous, gauge-invariant, time-reflection-symmetric function of the plaquette holonomies of K × ℤ; no homogeneous linear coefficient map can be equivariant (S24.3a), so (R1) is the canonical exact equivariant choice. **Reported against interest:** the product complex fixes only the **support** of the variables — it forces neither edge-additivity (**retracted, S24-R9**) nor time-locality (**retracted, S24-R11**), and a negative control exhibits an action built from two-slice variables that is **non-Markov in time**. **Time-locality is then derived, not assumed (Theorem S24.12):** under (R1) every configuration variable is slice- or slab-supported, so if the master Lagrangian is local with **no time derivatives beyond first order** — hypothesis (S1), which the ZS-S14 action satisfies term by term — then ∫dt splits over slabs and, after integrating slab-interior variables against a slab-factorised measure (S2), the partition function is an **exact one-step transfer product**. **The gap, in tiers (Theorem S24.9):** (i) the one-step kernel is the exponential of a real continuous function, hence continuous, symmetric and pointwise strictly positive on the compact **M**, so T is Hilbert–Schmidt, self-adjoint, gauge-commuting and positivity improving, and Perron–Frobenius gives a simple top eigenvalue with strictly positive eigenvector — the **correlation-decay gap is strictly positive**; (ii) if T is additionally positive and injective on the physical subspace — **(R2⁺)**, equivalently reflection positivity with a non-degenerate form — then H \= −log T is self-adjoint and **Δ\_phys ∈ (0, ∞)**. **The constructive step (Theorem S24.14): (R2⁺) is not imported but realised from the corpus's own data.** For any L satisfying (E) ∧ (G) and any bounded gauge-invariant V, the slab family **T\_a \= e^{−aV/2}e^{−aL}e^{−aV/2} \= S\*S** is manifestly positive, injective, compact, positivity improving with a continuous strictly positive kernel and gauge-commuting; the multi-slab measure it generates is **reflection positive by construction**; and −(1/a)log T\_a → L \+ V by symmetric Trotter in operator norm, so the finitely many lowest eigenvalues converge. In coupling-scaled form the family matches H\_g exactly. Hence **the ZS-S14 finite reduction admits a canonical reflection-positive symmetric-semigroup realisation, and tier (ii) holds for it without any appeal to the Wilson action**. **The exact electric-limit spectrum and its first-order structure:** beyond Δ\_{E,1} \= (10/3)g² with d₁ \= 24 and Δ\_{E,2} \= 4g² with d₂ \= 40 (Theorem S24.4), Proposition S24.15′ resolves the magnetic operator on the first eigenspace for an arbitrary real class function — every non-trivial character component vanishes within P₁ except on the plaquette itself, where the diagonal receives only the **1** and **8** characters and the orientation flip only the **3** and **6̄** — giving spectrum c₀ ± |μ| each 12-fold, so **the 24-fold level splits 24 → 12 ⊕ 12 if and only if μ ≠ 0**, with each 12-dimensional sector carrying A ⊕ T₁ ⊕ T₂ ⊕ H under I ≅ A₅ and the Wilson form recovered as the special case μ \= −κ₅/6; the asymptotic gap is Δ(g) \= (10/3)g² \+ \[a₈⁽⁵⁾ − |μ|\]g⁻² \+ O(g⁻⁶). **The v1.6 claim that P₁V\_BP₁ vanished is RETRACTED (S24-R12): it contracted the bra without conjugation.** **What remains:** gate **F-S24.18** (whether the exact Whitney-integrated ZS-S14 slab action coincides with a member of the canonical family T\_a, or is otherwise reflection positive) and **F-S24.14** (edge-additivity, affecting only the differential picture); **F-S24.19** ((S1)) is closed by inspection of the ZS-S14 Lagrangian. Weak coupling is unchanged: Inv(**8**) \= 0 while Inv(**8** ⊗ **8**) \= 1, so the candidate is 2√(rλ₁) (Proposition S24.6). Companion checks distinguish **control** (does a hypothesis hold automatically?) from **confirmation** (a finite check of a statement proved over an infinite range), and **neither is evidence for a physical value**. → ZS-S24 v1.9 \[100 ledger entries\]; the finite-carrier gap theorem for uniformly elliptic gauge-commuting L, the constructed reflection-positive slab family realising (R2⁺), and the exact electric-limit spectrum with the 24 → 12 ⊕ 12 first-order splitting criterion.

PART XXI totals approximately \+1,862 individual verification-ledger entries, advancing the cumulative physics-verification ledger from \~5,480+ (v11.0) to **\~7,340+ PASS**. *\[v12.1 update: the v12.0 figures were \+1,810 and \~7,290+, computed with ZS-U12 carried at registration level and therefore contributing no ledger entries; the supplied ZS-U12 v2.3 source body adds its 52/52 structural ledger (13 categories A–M) with the 46/46 executable suite zs\_u12\_verify.py mapped onto it.\]* Coverage advances 180 → 202 source papers. *Counting discipline, adopted from ZS-S22 v1.5 §banner and applied Book-wide from v12.0 onward: ledger entries are not theorem counts; where a source separates executable checks, computed diagnostics, proxies and declarations, the Book carries that separation rather than flattening it.*

**§XXI.C — v12.0 Cross-Coupling Discipline (T7) Compliance**

Per T7 (§VIII.7), every cross-paper synthesis is P-CC (PROVEN-composed) or I-CC (interpretation). All twenty-two PART XXI chapter entries are summarisations of source-paper Verification Summaries; no new theorem is introduced by PART XXI itself, and each absorbed status (PROVEN / PROVEN-CONDITIONAL / DERIVED / DERIVED-CONDITIONAL / DERIVED-PERT-COND / IMPORTED-PROVEN / IMPORTED-MECHANISM / COMPUTED / CERTIFIED / VERIFIED / LOCAL-NORMAL-FORM / SYNTHESIS-HYPOTHESIS / DISTINCT-CONSTRUCTION / HYPOTHESIS-strong / HYPOTHESIS-weak / OBSERVATION / IDENTITY / DECLARATION / CLOSED-NEGATIVE / CLOSED-PASS / SUPERSEDED-BY-CONSTRUCTION / OPEN / OPEN-TERMINAL / NON-CLAIM / RETRACTED) is preserved verbatim from the source. **New status vocabulary registered by PART XXI** (and used here only as the sources use it): **IMPORTED-MECHANISM** (ZS-F46 — an external *mechanism* developed in the literature under stated assumptions, not a settled theorem, consumed as a framework and never claimed as proven physics), **COMPUTED** (ZS-S23 — a number from an approximate prescription whose discretisation error is measured but not rigorously bounded, carrying **no proof weight**), **CLOSED-PASS** (ZS-S23 — a falsification gate executed that did not fire), **SUPERSEDED-BY-CONSTRUCTION** (ZS-S21 — an open search space eliminated because the construction shows there is no family to search), and **SYNTHESIS-HYPOTHESIS** (ZS-F47 — a proposed unification whose components are not shown logically identical).

The cardinal-2 substrate (dim(**Z**) \= 2), the register **Q** \= 11, and the locked (**A**, z\*, λ₁) acquire further corpus instantiations via PART XXI: the dimensionless charge residual ê₆ \= 2π e^{−4π**Q**} and the identification of four residual names with one Koenigs length (ZS-F43); the exact rational η\_Z \= (3/2)Ω\_Λ,0 \= 249/242 and the N \= 3 ⇔ dim **X** two-ledger classification (ZS-F45); the one-dimensional kernel of the 38-node seam graph Laplacian and the ½ ln 2 trace normalizer coinciding with the ZS-F19.6 handshake (ZS-F46); the icosahedral ℚ(√5) character field forcing the C₂³ carrier with conductors {1, 3, 5, 11, 15, 33, 55, 165} (ZS-M49); the ℕˣ-saturation ℚ/ℤ and the finite-register isometry no-go that explains *why* **Q** \= 11 cannot carry the Bost–Connes completion (ZS-M50); the Dottie threshold s\_c \= e^{sin ρ} recovering n\_c \= 3.20356751489 exactly and placing the **first contracting saddle at m \= 5, x₀ \= ¼** (ZS-M51); the spectrum {λ\_V, λ\_V, log m} as the arithmetic form of expansion–contraction complementarity (ZS-M52/F47); the exact mean-channel spectrum {1, 1, λ, λ̄} with |**λ**| \= (π/2)|z\*| (ZS-M53/Q18/U12); and the truncated-icosahedron register **Q** \= 11 \= 3 \+ 6 \+ 2 realised as the 90-edge / 32-face carrier whose Laplacian eigenvalues λ₁ and λ\_h are now known to be **algebraic integers of degree four with an exact minimal polynomial** whose root sum is 22 \= 2**Q** (ZS-S20).

The most consequential cross-paper structures are three. **(1) The four-route B3 terminus.** F43 (unit-invisibility), F44 (parent-factor non-determination), F45 (null-power reduction) and F46 (sequestering) reach the **same single residual** — a calibrated boundary datum, B3-B — from four logically independent directions; F44 additionally proves that residual **irreducibly external** rather than merely unreduced, and F46 separates cleanly what *is* addressed (the radiative-stability face of the old cosmological-constant problem, degravitated by the global seam constraint) from what is *not* (the residual value). Barrier B3 remains **not closed**, and Ω\_Λ,0 \= 83/121 and U\_N remain untouched and strictly separate. **(2) The Born rule as an i-tetration theorem.** The chain M51 → M52 → F47 → F48 → Q18 → M53 converts the corpus's oldest dynamical object — the i-tetration fixed point z\* and its multiplier — into a measurement theory: the genuine saddle (5, ¼) is located and certified (M51/M52), its local normal form and positive density-matrix intertwiner are constructed (F47/F48), the representative is corrected from amplitude-damping to **dephasing/QND** and the **Born rule is derived, unraveling-independently, for every purifying QND realization** (Q18), and the mean-channel spectrum is closed exactly (M53) — with the entire residual reduced to **one** named gate shared corpus-wide, the CRT-4/H-CLK clock equality, plus the physical instrument selector, explicitly **not** compressed into a single bedrock. **(3) The Yang–Mills carrier line as a model of negative-result discipline.** S17 → S24 is the corpus's longest sustained adversarial arc: S20 alone records **ten retractions from ten rounds of review**, six sharing one failure shape (a proxy reported as the target), and answers it by adding a PROXY check kind and static-analysis anti-regression to the companion; S21 refutes its own seed hypothesis twice and retracts its own unconditional σ \= 1; S22 warns in its banner that its own PASS count is not a theorem count; S23 rebuilds its ledger so that no entry is graded differently by manuscript and companion, and retracts a solver residual eleven orders of magnitude optimistic; and S24 retracts two of its own structural inferences (edge-additivity, time-locality) and then **derives time-locality properly** before constructing the reflection-positive realisation that closes the finite-carrier gap. The line ends **positively but narrowly**: a finite-carrier spectral gap with an explicit reflection-positive slab realisation, and an equally explicit non-claim that this is not a continuum SU(3) mass gap and not a Clay-form statement.

PART XXI contributes no new Banach–Tarski functor member. It registers the following **source-internal corrections and retractions of record**, each of which lives inside its source body and modifies no prior Book PART: the ZS-F44 v1.0 "Type III₁ ⇒ infinite Jones index ⇒ no trace" implication (RETRACTED, rebuilt on (H-NOTR)); the ZS-M48 retraction of the inherited channelwise operator theorem, of "the T9 remainder is independent of a", and the withdrawal of the earlier overreach that no operator built from g can be a route to RH; the ZS-M49 v1.1 "carrier must be infinite-dimensional / packet structure forced" withdrawal and the v1.2 odd-parity monotonicity gap closed in v1.3; the ZS-M50 \+½ Laplace-shift correction; the ZS-M52 v1.1 exponent-term mislabelling correction; the ZS-F47 downgrades (Linear CPTP Representative, h\_top not "e-fold", three-gaps SYNTHESIS-HYPOTHESIS); the ZS-F48 correction of the v1.4 global-skew obstruction and the g\_hf downgrade to OBSERVATION; the ZS-Q18 retraction of the "Type III₁ from entropy production" reading; the ZS-M53 withdrawal of non-quasi-compactness and the reset of G1–G5 to OPEN; the ZS-S17 correction of the 23% leakage figure to 7.14%; the ZS-S19 retraction of the −3.868% ledger statement, of its own v1.0 necessity claim, of the (R)-only counting-star derivation, of the Λ\_QCD band, and of the arithmetic-mean Hamiltonian relation; the ZS-S20 ten-retraction ledger with the anti-numerology MC reported negative (p \= 33.82%) and the erratum filed against ZS-S19 §1.1; the ZS-S21 retractions S21-R2 and S21-R3; the ZS-S22 retractions S22-R1 and S22-R2; the ZS-S23 retraction of "PROVEN by exhibition", of the v1.1 proof-strength grading, and of the 1.6 × 10⁻¹⁴ solver residual; and the ZS-S24 retractions S24-R9, S24-R11 and S24-R12. Cumulative no-deletion rule preserved across v3.0 → … → v11.0 → v12.0. Word count strictly increases over v11.0.

**§XXI.D — What v12.0 Does NOT Claim**

**NC-XXI.1.** v12.0 does NOT modify or retract any v11.0 chapter entry, count, or registry line. The supersedes statement is strictly additive; there are no v12.0 errata propagations into prior PARTS (all source-internal retractions listed in §XXI.C live inside those source bodies and are reported, not re-applied to earlier Book text).

**NC-XXI.2.** v12.0 does NOT supersede the v5.0–v11.0 PART X–XX readings; the Spin-Pair Coexistence Principle, the TO/TI Polyhedral Mapping, the PART XII–XIV absorptions, the PART XV ZS-T11 auditor, the PART XVI quantum-channel-geometry cluster, the PART XVII Lawvere/z\* cluster, the PART XVIII RH-bridge / ZHCS clusters, the PART XIX vacuum-scale / reality-structure clusters, and the PART XX charge-unit terminus are inherited unchanged.

**NC-XXI.3. Barrier B3 is still NOT closed, and no PART XXI paper claims otherwise.** ZS-F43 re-types the residual without closing it; ZS-F44 closes the parent-factor C\_UV route **CLOSED-NEGATIVE** and proves the residual irreducibly external; ZS-F45 is an **audit reaching the same wall**, with F-F40.6 armed and **not fired**; ZS-F46 addresses only the **radiative-stability face** and explicitly NON-CLAIMS the absolute value, leaving Λ\_res as a single calibrated boundary datum. The conditional value ê₆ \= 2π e^{−4π**Q**} inherits the ZS-A32 statistical status (HYPOTHESIS-strong, pre-registered MC) and performs **no mechanism promotion**; the firewalled C\_UV \= 1.244 is never counted as PASS evidence. Ω\_Λ,0 \= 83/121 (ZS-A30) and U\_N (ZS-A29) are separate and untouched.

**NC-XXI.4. No claim on RH or GRH is made or advanced.** ZS-M48 NC-1 and ZS-M49 NC-M49.1 are carried verbatim: global positivity of the Weil functional on the full admissible test space **is** GRH(ζ\_K), and the finite-grid, finite-dimensional and certified-enclosure results are unconditional but do not imply it. The ZS-M48 Galois-graded operator is a **numerical preconditioner**, not a Hilbert–Pólya operator; ZS-M49's certificate is a zero-**exclusion** certificate on a finite interval; ZS-M50's Bost–Connes representation is **covariant but not proved faithful or universal**, and POME-K′ is registered as "carrier and holonomy closed, weighted measure conditional", explicitly **not** "closed". Gate D5 remains IMPORTED-OPEN ≡ RH throughout, unchanged from PART XVIII.

**NC-XXI.5. The measurement arc does not claim a single-world ontology, a physical instrument selection, or a global transfer-operator spectrum.** ZS-Q18 derives the Born rule **for any purifying QND realization**; the *selection* of the physical instrument stays OPEN, ZS-Q16 strong sufficiency stays OPEN (and non-epistemic), and single-world ontology remains a NON-CLAIM. ZS-F47's central bridge is HYPOTHESIS and its three-gaps reduction a SYNTHESIS-HYPOTHESIS; ZS-F48's global resonances and fibre Lyapunov sign are OPEN (neither obstructed nor contracting-on-average), and its local mode set is explicitly **not** claimed to be a transfer-operator spectrum; ZS-M53's G1–G5 geometric programme is entirely OPEN. The corpus-wide residual is **two** distinct items — the CRT-4/H-CLK clock equality and the physical instrument selector — and v12.0 does not merge them.

**NC-XXI.6. The Yang–Mills line does not claim a continuum SU(3) mass gap, a Clay-form result, or a closed Yang–Mills bridge.** ZS-S21's scope declaration is carried Book-wide: "Yang–Mills bridge" there denotes the **cellular transfer-matrix / Hodge-measure sub-bridge only**. ZS-S20 leaves the physical selection ZS-S14 ⇒ (H-ALG) ∧ (H-TR) **NOT PROVEN** (both HYPOTHESIS-strong), so the bridge is DERIVED-CONDITIONAL; λ₁ closes conditionally while **Λ\_QCD \= 264.1 MeV does not** (F-S19.3 untouched). ZS-S22 establishes no continuum measure and no mass gap; ZS-S23 leaves the selection half OPEN at the named density-versus-rate identification, with the ZS-F40 √2 CLOSED-NEGATIVE clock discharge **unreconciled**; and ZS-S24's gap theorem is a **finite-carrier** statement whose application to the exact Whitney-integrated ZS-S14 slab action is gate **F-S24.18, OPEN**. g\_hf remains DERIVED-CONDITIONAL (ZS-S17 gate F-S17.7) and its λ₁ identification an OBSERVATION (ZS-F48), not an independent prediction.

**NC-XXI.7. Registration-level entries are flagged as such.** ZS-U12 v2.3 is registered at title-and-interface level because its source body is not present in this build; the Book asserts for it only what the citing papers (ZS-F47, ZS-F48, ZS-Q18, ZS-A6) assert, restates no unread verification ledger, and flags the chapter for upgrade to full source-derived level when the body is supplied. *\[v12.1: DISCHARGED. The ZS-U12 v2.3 source body has been supplied and Chapter 107 is now a full source-derived entry with its 52/52 structural and 46/46 executable ledger. The registration-level flag is retired, and the one substantive error it permitted — the v12.0 entry's item (iii), which carried the C\_ℓ ∝ |z|^{2ℓ} / ≈10% quadrupole template that ZS-U12 §11.2 itself RETRACTS — is corrected in Chapter 107 and in the Glossary, with the retracted statement retained and marked rather than deleted. No other v12.0 registration-level entry exists.\]*\* Three index/source version discrepancies are recorded of record (see §XXI.E) and resolved in favour of the source body.
