the ZS-F47 COND 1b-local (closed) / global Rokhlin extension (OPEN) and the Gap A/B/C synthesis gates; the ZS-F48 gates F-F48.9–F-F48.11 including the fibre-exponent sign χ\_f and the pre-registered out-of-sample hyperfine channels (1⁺⁻, 0⁻⁺, 2⁻⁺); the ZS-Q18 anti-numerology gate F-Q18.6 (Born fit χ²/dof \= 0.68, alternatives rejected) and the CRT-4/H-CLK clock-equality gate; the ZS-M53 OPEN set M53.6C and G1–G5; the ZS-S17 gate F-S17.7 (the Hessian ¼); the ZS-S18 metric-weight gate F-S18.16 (superseded as a comparison by ZS-S19) and the running-coupling matching gate; the ZS-S19 twenty-one gates across three layers, including F-S19.6 as a four-outcome decision gate, the defect-identity gate F-S19.A/A′, and the point-prediction gate F-S19.5; the ZS-S20 gate F-S20.8 **executed and FIRED**, F-S20.5 (later superseded by ZS-S21), and the executed anti-numerology MC (p \= 33.82%, negative); the ZS-S21 gate F-S21.12 (no orbit-weight selection attributable to the transfer matrix); the ZS-S22 conditions (C1) ∧ (Z-A2) ∧ (C3) and the audited-range escape statement; the ZS-S23 gates F-S23.9 (**CLOSED-PASS**, the first in the corpus) and F-S23.12 (metric-distance reinterpretation), with (Z-A3) newly named; and the ZS-S24 gates F-S24.14, F-S24.18 (OPEN) and F-S24.19 (closed by inspection). The **defining open gates of PART XXI** are three: the single calibrated boundary datum **B3-B**; the **CRT-4 / H-CLK clock equality**; and **F-S24.18**, the reflection positivity of the exact Whitney-integrated ZS-S14 slab action.

*Note of record 1 — index/source version discrepancies.* The supplied 181–202 index differs from the source bodies in three places, resolved here in favour of the source: paper 196 is listed as "ZS-S18 v2.3" carrying the ZS-S17 title, whereas the source is **ZS-S18 v1.6 FINAL, *The Symmetric Two-Body Sector of the Z-Spin Master Action*** (the duplicated title in the index is a transcription artifact); paper 198 is listed as ZS-S20 v2.1, source **v2.2**; paper 202 is listed as ZS-S24 v1.4, source **v1.9 FINAL**. No content depends on the resolution, but the Book records both readings so the discrepancy is auditable.

*Note of record 2 — the reused ZS-M48 code.* ZS-F43 v1.1 pre-registered two continuations, one of which reserved the code **ZS-M48** for a Parent-Factor Realization Execution; ZS-F44 v1.0 then proved that execution to be a corollary of its own no-go and **absorbed the reserved mandate**, so no paper was written under that reservation. The code was subsequently issued to the present, unrelated **ZS-M48 v2.7** (Suzuki channels). Both facts are recorded so that a reader meeting "ZS-M48" in ZS-F43/F44 (a reserved-and-absorbed mandate) and in ZS-M49/M50 (the Suzuki-channel paper) is not misled; this is the same class of nomenclature event as the ZS-F41 code re-issue recorded in PART XX and the ZS-Q17 → ZS-Q18 collision avoidance recorded in Chapter 115\.

*Note of record 4 \[v12.1\] — the ZS-U12 v2.3 anti-numerology execution status.* The ZS-U12 source is internally inconsistent on one point, recorded here rather than silently resolved: §11.3 states of the pre-registered anti-numerology Monte Carlo (17-element Z-invariant basis, rate factors {1, √6/**A**, 2π/**A**}, tolerance 10⁻³ on ln g per ℓ, seed 20260605\) that **"Execution: PENDING"**, and NC-U12.8 repeats that it is "pre-registered but NOT executed"; the Acknowledgements and the Appendix A ledger (category J) both report it **executed at p \= 1.40%**. The Book carries the ledger as the paper's own audited artifact while flagging the §11.3 / NC-U12.8 text as stale relative to it, and reads the discrepancy in the conservative direction: the *form* (power-law cutoff, Thm U12.3) is DERIVED independently of any MC, while the numerical exponent n\_supp ≈ 7.02 remains DERIVED-CONDITIONAL on λ\_vac \= 2**A**² under NC-U12.7 in either reading. A second, purely bookkeeping item: gate **F-U12.7** is announced in the ZS-U12 v2.2 version-history line (the sharp-cutoff / erasure-notch gate) but does not appear as a row in the consolidated Table 5, which runs F-U12.1–F-U12.6 and F-U12.8; its content is carried by F-U12.4.

*Note of record 3 — the ZS-M48 v2.7 provenance flag.* The source states plainly that the analytic blocks were executed to completion in isolation but that a single-process run of all 380 checks exceeded the drafting environment's interactive limit, so the full stdout, JSON ledger, library versions, script SHA-256 and wall-clock time are **not yet included** and are required before public release. The Book carries the ledger with that flag attached and does not treat the 380 count as an executed-and-published certificate.

**§XXI.F — Conclusion**

With PART XXI the Light Edition is the canonical **202-paper** navigation hub for the complete Z-Spin Cosmology corpus. PART XXI is, in character, the corpus's **construction-and-audit increment**: where PART XX localized barrier B3 to a single residual, PART XXI attacks that residual from three further independent directions and finds the same wall each time — then says so plainly, four times over, and converts the effort into reusable tools (the η\_Z identity, the seam-as-null-energy channel, the A-vs-ω discriminator, the sequestering seam action) rather than into a claimed closure. Alongside it the corpus gains its first genuinely external-facing mathematics — Suzuki's finite-interval operator transferred to every real primitive Dirichlet channel, a cheap rigorous zero-exclusion certificate for quadratic Dirichlet L-functions released as a self-contained note, and the exact Lambert–Dottie threshold s\_c \= e^{sin ρ} with its certified fixed-point census — all of it careful to claim nothing about RH. It gains, in the F47 → F48 → Q18 → M53 chain, what may be its most consequential physical result to date: **the Born rule derived from i-tetration**, unraveling-independently, once the representative is corrected from amplitude-damping to dephasing/QND — with the residual honestly left as two distinct named gates rather than one bedrock. And it gains, in S17 → S24, a finite-carrier Yang–Mills gap theorem with a **constructed** reflection-positive realisation, arrived at through the most adversarial review sequence the corpus has recorded — ten retractions in one paper, self-refutation of seed hypotheses in another, a ledger rebuilt so that manuscript and companion cannot disagree in a third — and bounded by an equally explicit statement of what it is not. Barrier B3 remains **not closed**; RH and GRH remain untouched; the Yang–Mills bridge remains DERIVED-CONDITIONAL at the cellular sub-bridge scope; and (**A**, **Q**, dim(**Z**)) \= (35/437, 11, 2\) remains LOCKED across all 202 source papers.

**v3.2 PART X falsification gates (added):**

• F-X.1. If a future analysis exhibits a prime p such that W\_p|\_Z does not take the σ\_z-rotation form on the {|4⟩, |6⟩} basis, Reading X.1 fails. Status: PROVEN-safe (verified for p ∈ {2,3,5,7,11,13,17,23} via ZS-M18 §H1).

• F-X.2. If a future ZS-F18 revision retracts the Y-side classification of the infinite prime sequence, Reading X.2 fails. Status: stable (over-determined by ZS-F18 §5.1 Tbl 5.1, §7.4.5).

• F-X.3. Synthesis-layer gate; weakened only if any of M1, M2, M3 is shown structurally independent of Z-Spin mediation. M3 conditional on RH. Status: DERIVED-interpretation, robust against single-component perturbation.

• F-X.4. If a contradiction-pair is found that admits coexistence under a phase difference other than π/2, Reading X.4 weakens to “90° is the dominant case”. If no Z-Spin J-conjugate realisation exists for some pair, X.4 transitions to “covers the 13 realisations C1–C13”. Status: DERIVED-interpretation strong; F-X.4 pre-registered for any future contradiction-pair claim.

**v3.5 PART X readings and falsification gates (added):**

• Reading X.5 (Apollonian as 5th Bridge-Four Instance, DERIVED-strong, NEW v3.5). The integer Apollonian gasket with curvature root (−1, 2, 2, 3\) is the fifth instance of ZS-F18 §6.5 Bridge Four (Commutator Generator) and the first realization on the integer lattice (ℤ, \+). The four prior instances — (1) ZS-S15 §3 Twin-Reuleaux EM duality, (2) ZS-M3 Lemma 10.1 SU(2) center pairing, (3) ZS-M32 §5.2 spinor-cycle averaging, (4) ZS-F4 §7B half-angle pair — all live on continuous spinor algebras. The Apollonian instance demonstrates that Bridge Four extends to discrete number theory: the abelianization (ℤ/2)\*4 → (ℤ/2)⁴ carries V₄ × V₄ structure unifying ZS-A9 register V₄ and ZS-M22 Galois V₄. → ZS-M36 §10.3 Table 10.1, ZS-F18 §6.5 \[DERIVED-strong\].

• Reading X.6 (mod 24 Spin Pair \= Axiom A4 Two Faces, DERIVED-interpretation strong, NEW v3.5). The eight admissible mod 24 residues of the (−1, 2, 2, 3\) gasket form four (algebraic, geometric) pairs — Pair Z {2, 14}, Pair X {3, 15}, Pair Y {6, 18}, Pair Q {11, 23} — in bijection with the four Z-Spin sectors. The mod 12 representatives (2, 3, 6, 11\) are the bosonic SO(3) sector dimensions; the \+12 shifted representatives (14, 15, 18, 23\) are the fermionic SU(2) geometric instantiations (F(t-Oct), X · |I\_h/T\_d|, α in degrees, δ\_Y denominator). The mod 24 \= 2 × mod 12 doubling is forced by ZS-M3 Lemma 10.1 PROVEN (D^{1/2}(2π) \= −I) and realizes axiom A4 (Non-triviality) algebraic/geometric two faces of ZS-F18 §6.6 Table 6.1 PROVEN-DERIVED. CRT decomposition: mod 24 \= mod 8 × mod 3 \= Z³ · X (boson 2π / fermion 4π periods, ZS-M1 §6 PROVEN). → ZS-M36 §10.5–10.6, ZS-F18 §6.6 \[DERIVED-interpretation strong\].

• F-X.5. If a future revision retracts the (−1, 2, 2, 3\) ↔ (Z, X, Y, Q) mod 12 identity (e.g., if a counterexample primitive integer ACP is found with mod 12 ≠ {2, 3, 6, 11} but with the same admissible mod 24 type), Reading X.5 fails. Status: DERIVED-strong, robust against single-counterexample (test: enumerate primitive roots up to depth 25, verify mod 12 set stable; ZS-M36 verification suite Categories C, H, 12/12 PASS at 500K MC).

• F-X.6. If the (algebraic, geometric) pair correspondence of M36.6 fails for any of the four Z-Spin sectors (e.g., if 18° frame mismatch angle is shown not to be a corpus-LOCKED quantity, or if F(t-Oct) \= 14 is retracted), Reading X.6 weakens from DERIVED-interpretation strong to OBSERVATION. Status: DERIVED-interpretation strong; pre-registered as a watchtower for future ZS-F2, ZS-S15, or ZS-S1 revisions. (Affecting Theorem M36.6 of ZS-M36; ZS-M36 Falsification Gate F-M36.6 mirrors this gate at the source-paper level.)

• Closure note. ZS-M36 Theorem M36.5 (Three V₄ Bridge, DERIVED-strong) closes ZS-M27 OPEN problem O-M27.4 (“Two V₄ groups: D₄ register vs Galois unification”) via the Apollonian abelianization (ℤ/2)\*4 → V₄ × V₄, with V₄\_register \= ⟨\[S₀\], \[S₁\]⟩ ↔ ZS-A9 ⟨J, J\_Z⟩ D₄ subgroup, and V₄\_galois \= ⟨\[S₂\], \[S₃\]⟩ ↔ ZS-M22 Gal(K/ℚ) for K \= ℚ(√−3, √−11). Status: O-M27.4 OPEN → CLOSED at DERIVED-strong (v3.5).

| ê₆ (dimensionless membrane charge) ✤ | ê₆ := e₆/M̄\_P², the well-posed residual of barrier B3; conditional value 2π e^{−4π**Q**} ≈ 5.83 × 10⁻⁶⁰ at the ZS-F38 depth | ZS-F43 §T2 (Ch 103\) |
| One-Residual Uniformization ✤ | e₆, M\_UV, M\_K and the metric-scale gate are one object — the Koenigs linearizing length in Planck units ℓ̂ — with ê₆ \= 2π(ℓ̄\_P/E\_len(seam))² | ZS-F43 §T2 (Ch 103\) |
| (H-NOTR) ✤ | A Type III factor has no tracial state (Connes classification, consumed not derived); the correct, index-independent premise of the Parent-Factor Non-Determination | ZS-F44 §3.2 (Ch 104\) |
| Parent-Factor Non-Determination ✤ | No admissible parent-factor realization fixes C\_UV ∈ \[1.0, 1.6\]; the parent-factor route to the absolute scale is CLOSED-NEGATIVE | ZS-F44 Cor. M48 (Ch 104\) |
| Kernel-Factorization Theorem ✤ | C\_UV \= \[c\_χ/(1260/4807)·e^{8π**Q**}\]·(H\_∂/M̄\_P)²: a dimensionless kernel 3.5739 × 10¹²⁰ times exactly one metric-side datum (B3-B) | ZS-F44 §T3 (Ch 104\) |
| B3-B (the single calibrated boundary datum) ✤ | The one external metric-side datum to which four independent routes (F33/F42/F43, F44, F45, F46) reduce barrier B3; proven irreducibly external, not merely unreduced | ZS-F44 §5.2 / ZS-F46 §T4 (Ch 104, 106\) |
| Null-Power Reduction Theorem ✤ | The null-boundary/Planck-power route's value-carrying content reduces to the pre-existing ê₆ ≡ E\_len(seam) residual; a fourth independent line reaching the same wall | ZS-F45 Thm F45.R (Ch 105\) |
| η\_Z \= 249/242 ✤ | The exact Planck-power/frozen-gas identity constant, η\_Z \= (3/2)Ω\_Λ,0 \= ¼c\_χω² (ω² cancels rationally); IDENTITY, recorded so it is not mistaken for evidence | ZS-F45 §2.1 T1 (Ch 105\) |
| Two-ledger reading (N \= 3 ⇔ dim **X**) ✤ | The Planck force's ℏ-exponent vanishes iff the spatial dimension is 3, so the IR frozen-gas ledger is the ℏ-free (G, c) ledger, mirror to the G-free charge unit q\_P | ZS-F45 §2.1 (Ch 105\) |
| **A**\-vs-ω Holonomy Discriminator ✤ | A Chern–Simons deficit is a per-cycle *additive* increment, so only the telomere drift **A** qualifies as a holonomy; ω \= 2.2592 is the helical twist Re τ\_K | ZS-F45 §T3 (Ch 105\) |
| Seam sequestering constraint ✤ | On the connected 38-node ZS-A19 seam graph dim ker L\_Γ \= 1, giving exactly one global, offset-sensitive parent charge — the degree of freedom a sequestering multiplier requires | ZS-F46 §T2 (Ch 106\) |
| Finite-register trace normalizer (½ ln 2\) ✤ | The ZS-F23 canonical trace fixes the Type II additive constant at ½ ln 2, coinciding with the ZS-F19.6 seam handshake gap; template DERIVED-CONDITIONAL, vacuum application PROGRAMMATIC | ZS-F46 §T3 (Ch 106\) |
| Multiplier-Selection Theorem (U12.1) ✤ | |**λ**| \= (π/2)|z\*|, the transfer multiplier consumed without re-fitting by ZS-Q18/F47/F48 and matching the ZS-M1 leaky Wilson loop and ZS-M43 leak 0.2052 | ZS-U12 §U12.1 (Ch 107\) |
| z\*-locked low-ℓ suppression ✤ | ~~C\_ℓ ∝ |z\*|^{2ℓ} on superhorizon modes, giving ≈ 10% quadrupole suppression~~ **RETRACTED by ZS-U12 §11.2** (the fixed-point *location* is not an admissible multiplier); superseded by the Koenigs ladder |**λ**|^{2ℓ} and the continuous cutoff (k/k\_b)^{n\_supp} | ZS-U12 §11.2 / ZS-A6 §7.2 (Ch 107\) |
| Channel Perturbation Theorem ✤ | h\_χ \= g\_χ − g\_ζ has the archimedean 1/(2|x|) singularity cancelled, so h\_χ″ is a finite signed measure with δ₀-mass −log q\_χ; Suzuki's ζ operator theorem transfers to every real primitive Dirichlet channel | ZS-M48 §8.2 T11 (Ch 108\) |
| Galois-graded operator 𝔄\_a ✤ | The canonical finite direct sum with one Suzuki block per element of Ĝ, G-equivariant and Ĝ-graded; a numerical preconditioner, not a Hilbert–Pólya operator | ZS-M48 §8.3 T12 (Ch 108\) |
| C₂³ quasicrystal carrier ✤ | L \= ℚ(√−3, √−11, √5) with Gal(L/ℚ) ≅ C₂³, eight real primitive characters of conductors {1, 3, 5, 11, 15, 33, 55, 165}, disc(L) \= 165⁴; the √5 forced by End\_{ℚA₅} \= ℚ(√5) | ZS-M49 §(i) (Ch 109\) |
| Theta–Lipschitz zero-exclusion certificate ✤ | A t-independent Lipschitz constant M₁(χ) with closed-form bound gives a covering grid certifying no zero **of any multiplicity** on \[0, T\] at a few hundred ball evaluations per channel | ZS-M49 Thm M49.C / C1 (Ch 109\) |
| POME-K′ ✤ | Deninger carrier \+ Artin-symbol holonomy IMPORTED-PROVEN; the weighted measure needs (P-TRANS) and (P-HALF) and is DERIVED-CONDITIONAL — "carrier and holonomy closed, measure conditional", not "POME-K′ closed" | ZS-M49 §(iii) (Ch 109\) |
| Horizontal direction (base dilation) ✤ | b ↦ b^m, an expanding cyclotomic endomorphism (not a rotation), whose roof is the additive log-time cocycle τ(m) \= log m | ZS-M50 §T1/T4 (Ch 110\) |
| ℕˣ-saturation ℚ/ℤ ✤ | The minimal ℕˣ-invariant base generated by the polygon seeds; a semigroup saturation, not a topological closure — the canonical arithmetic base of the horizontal clock | ZS-M50 §T2 (Ch 110\) |
| Covariant Bost–Connes representation ✤ | The explicit μ\_n, e(r), H representation on ℓ²(ℕˣ) discharging gate H-ALG (PROVEN); covariant, with faithfulness explicitly not claimed | ZS-M50 §7 (Ch 110\) |
| Finite-register isometry No-Go ✤ | In any finite-dimensional register V\*V \= I forces V unitary, so a proper isometry cannot exist — the Bost–Connes completion is necessarily infinite-dimensional and **Q** \= 11 cannot carry it | ZS-M50 Thm M50.NG (Ch 110\) |
| Dottie threshold s\_c \= e^{sin ρ} ✤ | The exact critical stability threshold of f\_s(z) \= e^{isz}, ρ \= cos ρ \= 0.7390851332…, s\_c \= 1.9613088464595…; gives n\_c \= 2π e^{−sin ρ} \= 3.20356751489… exactly | ZS-M51 Thm T2 (Ch 111\) |
| General Lambert Mean Identity ✤ | (1/X)∫₀^X log|W₀(−2πix)|dx \= log|W\_X| − Re W\_X/|W\_X|²; its two evaluations give the band mean −sin ρ and the frozen section mean Λ\_sec | ZS-M51 Thm T3 (Ch 111\) |
| Fixed-Point Census ✤ | Interior base-fixed points give saddles of stable index 2 below x\_c and 3D repellers above; count N\_m \= ⌈x\_c(m−1)⌉ − 1, first contracting saddle at m \= 5, x₀ \= ¼ | ZS-M51 Thm T5–T6 (Ch 111\) |
| Krawczyk-certified coexistence ✤ | Rigorous interval certification of a locally unique attracting cycle (1/7; m \= 2\) and repelling cycle (1/3; m \= 2), closing H-UFC and H-2s1u CLOSED-NEGATIVE while H-PH stays OPEN | ZS-M51 Thm T7 (Ch 111\) |
| Full Periodic Lyapunov Spectrum ✤ | {λ\_V, λ\_V, log m} for a cyclotomic word; λ\_V \= K\_x − 2πJ\_x (fibre kinematic loading minus fibre amplitude contraction), χ\_H \= log m the true horizontal exponent | ZS-M52 Thm M52.1A (Ch 112\) |
| Arithmetic coherence / return time ✤ | λ\_V \< 0 \< log m (an exact 2s1u saddle), equivalently J\_x \> K\_x/2π; coherence return time q \= ord\_n(m) | ZS-M52 Cor. M52.1.1 / M52.2 (Ch 112\) |
| Seam-Matching No-Go ✤ | No continuous seam-compatible invariant graph exists for the canonical \[0,1)-representative cocycle (seam closure forces m·N₁ \= 1, impossible for m ≥ 2); closes the continuous category of H-INV | ZS-M52 Thm M52.6A (Ch 112\) |
| Large-Excursion Gate ✤ | The honest holding of the continuum: 18 irrational-base trajectories reach |z| \> 10¹² with non-monotone returns, from which **neither** unboundedness **nor** divergence is inferred | ZS-M52 §M52.4 (Ch 112\) |
| Expansion–Contraction Complementarity ✤ | The proposal that Z-Spin's complementarities are the two signed projections of one arithmetic cocycle F\_m(z, x) \= (e^{2πixz}, {mx}); central bridge HYPOTHESIS | ZS-F47 §0 (Ch 113\) |
| Genuine arithmetic saddle (5, ¼) ✤ | The base-fixed coherent word at which the full cocycle is closed as the factor of an invertible **local** extension (COND 1b-local PROVEN); global extension OPEN | ZS-F47 §COND 1b (Ch 113\) |
| SYNTHESIS-HYPOTHESIS ✤ | Status tag: a proposed unification whose components are not shown logically identical (here Gaps A, B, C sharing the parent candidate ZS-S14) | ZS-F47 Table L (Ch 113\) |
| Saddle Diagonalization Lemma ✤ | The full-cocycle Jacobian at the saddle is \[\[λ, β\], \[0, 5\]\] with β \= 2πi(z\*)² ≠ 0, conjugate to diag(λ, 5); local algebraic mode set {λᵃλ̄ᵇ5⁻ᶜ} exact at first order | ZS-F48 §Lemma (Ch 114\) |
| Positive density-matrix intertwiner E(w) ✤ | E(w) \= \[\[1 − 2|w|², w̄\], \[w, 2|w|²\]\] on |w| ≤ ½ with Φ(E(w)) \= E(λw) EXACTLY and pointer E(z\*) \= |0⟩⟨0|; c \= 2 fixed by positivity-domain maximization, no free parameter | ZS-F48 §measurement (Ch 114\) |
| Hyperfine-coefficient gate (g\_hf) ✤ | √(1 \+ ¾g\_hf) from the Clebsch channel gate; lattice r \= 1.390 ± 0.039 requires g\_hf \= 1.24 ± 0.15, so g\_hf \= λ₁ is an OBSERVATION (a re-expression of the datum), not an independent prediction | ZS-F48 §glueball (Ch 114\) |
| Dephasing (QND) representative ✤ | The correct Koenigs mean channel Φ\_deph : ρ₁₀ ↦ **λ**ρ₁₀ with populations conserved and diagonal Kraus operators — replacing the amplitude-damping representative, which can never Born-select | ZS-Q18 §(i) (Ch 115\) |
| Born rule from i-tetration ✤ | For a σ\_z-QND channel every purifying unraveling gives P(|j⟩) \= ρ\_jj(0) (QND ⇒ population martingale; purification ⇒ p\_∞ ∈ {0,1}); unraveling-independent | ZS-Q18 Thm Q18.2 (Ch 115\) |
| CRT-4 / H-CLK clock equality ✤ | The single corpus-wide OPEN gate: the QND measurement-record clock equals the modular seam clock of the i-tetration Abel-cover unit translation; RH-free, with a degree-1-Blaschke candidate | ZS-Q18 §4.8 (Ch 115\) |
| Mean-channel spectrum {1, 1, λ, λ̄} ✤ | Φ \= diag(1, 1, λ̄, **λ**) for the λ-locked mean channel — full multiplicities and eigenspaces, unconditional for the defined channel; physical instrument selection OPEN | ZS-M53 Thm M53.4 (Ch 116\) |
| Instrument uniqueness (U(1)² × ℤ₂) ✤ | The informative two-outcome diagonal QND instrument is unique up to Kraus phases and outcome exchange, once symmetry, no detector bias and the mean-channel constraint hold; |**λ**| saturates the triangle bound | ZS-M53 Thm M53.7 (Ch 116\) |
| Two-T₁ alternating active space ✤ | T₁(λ₁) ⊕ T₁(λ\_h) closed under the alternating (Yang–Mills-relevant) cochain vertex with zero leakage; gap-only leakage 7.14% (correcting 23%), full bilinear leaks 29.5% | ZS-S17 §1 (Ch 117\) |
| Casimir-coproduct Layer-Lift ✤ | On T₁, C₂ \= 2I so L₂ \= (λ₁/2)C₂ and the coproduct defect is I\_Z \= λ₁S₁·S₂ exactly, giving M(2⁺⁺)²/M(0⁺⁺)² \= 1 \+ 3λ₁/4 \= 1.3900 | ZS-S17 §3 (Ch 117\) |
| Gate F-S17.7 (the Hessian ¼) ✤ | The one remaining physical step of the hyperfine line: whether the ZS-S14 mass-squared Hessian is exactly Q\_Z \= ¼(I\_Z \+ 2λ₁I) | ZS-S17 §3 (Ch 117\) |
| Harmonic anchoring identity ✤ | The exact identity of the symmetric two-body sector, audited against the Wilson holonomy; its defect form is closed by ZS-S19 Thm S19.2 | ZS-S18 §0 (Ch 118\) |
| Normalization Ambiguity (a\_TI) ✤ | The DEC face star carries dimension L⁻² and is defined only once an edge length is declared; a single rescaling sweeps λ₁ over (0, ∞), so the −3.868% metric alarm is retracted as a ledger statement | ZS-S19 Thm S19.1 (Ch 119\) |
| Anchoring Defect Identity ✤ | dev(λ) \= −\[W₅₆S₅₆(λ) \+ W₆₆S₆₆(λ)\] for every I\_h-invariant diagonal DEC metric and every eigen-channel; residual below the 10⁻¹² threshold | ZS-S19 Thm S19.2 (Ch 119\) |
| (R\_C) cellular counting trace ✤ | The explicit axiom replacing the retracted derivation of the counting star from (R) alone; strictly stronger, and the sole dependency of counting-star selection | ZS-S19 §4 (Ch 119\) |
| Regge-Moduli Exclusion ✤ | Q(t) \> 1 for every t \> 0 over the full two-dimensional I\_h-invariant Regge moduli, certified by outward-rounded interval arithmetic; excludes the diagonal circumcentric Regge-metric route | ZS-S19 Thm S19.6 (Ch 119\) |
| Non-identifiability of the Hodge measure ✤ | Nothing in the quadratic cellular action fixes (M₁, M₂): hermiticity defines the adjoint by M, every PD matrix is a Gram matrix, and the transfer Hessian returns the M put in | ZS-S20 Thm S20.O/N-a/T1 (Ch 120\) |
| (H-ALG) ∧ (H-TR) two-layer status ✤ | LAYER 1 arithmetic uniqueness ρ \= σ \= r \= 1 PROVEN over all positive reals; LAYER 2 the physical selection ZS-S14 ⇒ (H-ALG) ∧ (H-TR) NOT PROVEN — both HYPOTHESIS-strong | ZS-S20 §Headline (Ch 120\) |
| Minimal polynomial of λ₁, λ\_h ✤ | B₂B₂ᵀ factors over ℤ as λ(λ−6)⁴(λ−8)⁵(λ²−10λ+22)⁵(λ⁴−22λ³+166λ²−480λ+380)³; λ₁ and λ\_h are algebraic integers of degree four, root sum 22 \= 2**Q** (observation, no connecting theorem) | ZS-S20 §by-product (Ch 120\) |
| (H-W) Wilson-type carrier ✤ | The hypothesis that the cellular reduction is a group-valued plaquette action with compact links and nearest-neighbour time coupling; OPEN, and load-bearing for S21–S24 | ZS-S21 Thm S21.1 (Ch 121\) |
| SUPERSEDED-BY-CONSTRUCTION ✤ | Status tag: an open search space eliminated because the construction shows there is no family to search (here gate F-S20.5, the non-diagonal I\_h-equivariant family) | ZS-S21 Thm S21.1 (Ch 121\) |
| (Z-A0) / (Z-A1) ✤ | Metric-Free Carrier (removes dual-measure and area branches) and the Orbit-Blind Plaquette Reduction Postulate (removes ambient-combinatorial branches, both anisotropy classes); together they give σ \= ρ \= 1 | ZS-S21 §6.4 (Ch 121\) |
| ψ(n\_f) obstruction ✤ | β\_f \= β\_sψ(n\_f) is metric-free and realises every ρ \> 0, so a metric-free carrier alone does **not** force uniformity — discovered and retracted in ZS-S19 v1.3, recurring one level up in ZS-S21 | ZS-S21 §6.1 (Ch 121\) |
| Goldberg–Coxeter family K\_n \= GP(n,n) ✤ | The icosahedral refinement family with V\_n \= 60n², E\_n \= 90n², F\_n \= 30n² \+ 2, 12 pentagons and 60 (5,6) edges fixed while the hexagonal bulk grows | ZS-S22 Thm S22.1 (Ch 122\) |
| Defect-Support Theorem ✤ | Δ\_n(σ,ρ) − Δ\_n(1,1) is exactly supported on the closed graph neighbourhood of the defect set — a principal block of dimension 72, independent of n (sub-bounds 60 and 24\) | ZS-S22 Thm S22.2 (Ch 122\) |
| Two universality classes ✤ | Two inequivalent refinement limits of the ZS-S21 instrument, with the determination of which ZS-S21 hypothesis selects between them (the re-attribution of the orbit-blind postulate) | ZS-S22 §0.1 (Ch 122\) |
| Ledger entries ≠ theorem counts ✤ | The ZS-S22 counting discipline adopted Book-wide from v12.0: where a source separates executable checks, computed diagnostics, proxies and declarations, the Book carries that separation | ZS-S22 §banner (Ch 122\) |
| COMPUTED (status) ✤ | A number from an approximate numerical prescription whose discretisation error is measured but not rigorously bounded; **carries no proof weight** | ZS-S23 Legend (Ch 123\) |
| CLOSED-PASS (status) ✤ | A falsification gate that has been executed and did not fire (first instance: F-S23.9, the interval-certified metric separation) | ZS-S23 Legend / F-S23.9 (Ch 123\) |
| (Z-A3) clock-route axiom ✤ | The axiom under which ZS-F38's unique ergodicity transfers to the carrier to give σ \= ρ \= 1; objection stated, not buried — a stationary probability is not a kinetic stiffness | ZS-S23 Thm S23.3 (Ch 123\) |
| Central-Hessian Theorem ✤ | For compact simple G any C² central Φ has Ad-invariant identity-Hessian, hence by Schur κ\_Φ times the Killing form; centrality ⇔ gauge invariance for a single-plaquette term | ZS-S23 Thm S23.4 / Lemma S23.5 (Ch 123\) |
| Edge/face isospectrality correction ✤ | The Hessian is a 90 × 90 edge-space operator and Δ\_{S21} a 32 × 32 face-space operator: **nonzero-isospectral, not equal** (correcting a type error inherited from ZS-S22 v1.2) | ZS-S23 Thm S23.6 (Ch 123\) |
| Finite-carrier gap theorem ✤ | For uniformly elliptic (E), gauge-commuting (G) L and gauge-invariant V, H\_g \= g²L \+ g⁻²V has compact resolvent, a unique strictly positive ground state and a strictly positive gap for every g \> 0 | ZS-S24 Thm S24.2 (Ch 124\) |
| Reflection-positive slab family T\_a ✤ | T\_a \= e^{−aV/2}e^{−aL}e^{−aV/2} \= S\*S — positive, injective, compact, positivity improving, gauge-commuting; realises (R2⁺) **by construction** and converges to L \+ V by symmetric Trotter | ZS-S24 Thm S24.14 (Ch 124\) |
| (R1) canonical holonomy reduction ✤ | Link variables are the edge holonomies U\_e \= P exp ∫\_e A — the canonical exact equivariant choice, since no homogeneous linear coefficient map can be equivariant | ZS-S24 §4 / Prop S24.3a (Ch 124\) |
| Exact electric-limit spectrum ✤ | Δ\_{E,1} \= (10/3)g² (d₁ \= 24), Δ\_{E,2} \= 4g² (d₂ \= 40); the 24-fold level splits 24 → 12 ⊕ 12 iff μ ≠ 0, each sector carrying A ⊕ T₁ ⊕ T₂ ⊕ H under I ≅ A₅ | ZS-S24 Thm S24.4 / Prop S24.15′ (Ch 124\) |
| Gate F-S24.18 ✤ | Whether the exact Whitney-integrated ZS-S14 slab action coincides with a member of the canonical family T\_a, or is otherwise reflection positive; OPEN — the terminal gate of the S-line | ZS-S24 §What remains (Ch 124\) |

**Glossary Keyword Index — v12.1 Additions (ZS-U12 v2.3 source-body upgrade, Chapter 107\)**

The v12.1 Light Glossary appends the following entries, all from the ZS-U12 v2.3 source body supplied after the v12.0 build. ✦ marks v12.1 vocabulary. One v12.0 entry is corrected in place above (the retracted |z\*|^{2ℓ} template); no entry is deleted.

| Term | Definition (one line) | Canonical anchor |
| :---- | :---- | :---- |
| Multiplier-Selection Theorem ✦ | Koenigs (1884): the admissible per-transit factor is the *rate* |f′(z\*)| \= (π/2)|z\*| \= 0.8915136, not the *location* |z\*|; power survival |**λ**|² \= (π²/4)η\_topo \= 0.7947964 | ZS-U12 Thm U12.1 (Ch 107\) |
| Retraction of |z\*|⁴ ≈ 10% ✦ | The ZS-A6 §7.2 quadrupole identification C₂/C₂^ΛCDM ∝ |z\*|⁴ \= 0.10376 is RETRACTED as numerology-risk: inadmissible multiplier, and a single cosmic-variance-limited number against a fuzzy range | ZS-U12 §11.2 (Ch 107\) |
| Wilson-loop bounce-transfer object ✦ | Because the Z-Telomere bounce is topological (no smooth Mukhanov–Sasaki background), the transfer is the BV-BFV partition function Z(W) \= **λ**, n-cycle survival |**λ**|^{2n} | ZS-U12 Thm U12.2 (Ch 107\) |
| Total-wipeout paradox ✦ | Uniform per-cycle survival over N\_{2π} \= 2π/**A** ≈ 78.45 cycles would give the k-independent annihilation |**λ**|^{2N\_{2π}} ≈ 1.5 × 10⁻⁸, erasing all structure — the paradox that forces horizon-crossing imprint | ZS-U12 §6.1 (Ch 107\) |
| Horizon-crossing imprint / τ(k) ✦ | Superhorizon ζ conservation (Weinberg 2008 §5) means a frozen mode carries no phase for the loop to act on, so τ(k) \= ν\_c·ln(k\_b/k) and P\_Z/P \= (k/k\_b)^{n\_supp} for k \< k\_b | ZS-U12 Thm U12.3 (Ch 107\) |
| n\_supp \= (√6/**A**)(−ln|**λ**|²) ✦ | The canonical suppression exponent ≈ 7.02 from H\_b \= (**A**/√6)M\_P and ν\_c \= √6/**A** ≈ 30.58 cycles per e-fold; zero free parameters, natural-H\_b bracket \[0.230, 7.02, 18.0\] | ZS-U12 Thm U12.4 (Ch 107\) |
| −ln|**λ**|² \= 0.2296693 ✦ | The suppression-per-e-fold: the mimicry-resistant z\*-content of the observable, LOCKED and independent of the bounce energy scale | ZS-U12 §5, §12 (Ch 107\) |
| Leaky **Z**\-block monodromy ✦ | Superhorizon Floquet monodromy M\_f \= \[\[Re **λ**, −Im **λ**\], \[Im **λ**, Re **λ**\]\], det \= |**λ**|² \= 0.794796 \< 1 (non-symplectic); the leak 1 − |**λ**|² \= 0.2052 to the ℤ₂-odd channel **is** the suppression | ZS-U12 Thm U12.5 (Ch 107\) |
| Subhorizon symplectic limit ✦ | Deep-subhorizon the equation is an adiabatic oscillator with det \= 1, |M| \= 1 — subhorizon modes do not leak, as the adiabatic theorem requires | ZS-U12 Thm U12.5(b) (Ch 107\) |
| Hilltop near-de Sitter core ✦ | V″(0) \< 0 at ε \= 0 makes the topological core a topological-inflation phase at H\_b \= (**A**/√6)M\_P ≈ 0.0327 M\_P, spanning N\_core \= 2π/√6 ≈ 2.565 e-folds (dφ/dN \= √6) | ZS-U12 Thm U12.6 (Ch 107\) |
| de Sitter crossing window W(k) ✦ | W(k) \= 1 for k ≪ k\_b, → 0 for k ≫ k\_b, transition width ≈ 1 e-fold; full template P\_Z/P \= W(k)(k/k\_b)^{n\_supp} over Δln k ≈ 2.565 (factor ≈ 13 in k) | ZS-U12 Thm U12.7 (Ch 107\) |
| Erasure notch ✦ | Since e^{−n\_supp} ≈ 0.0009, modes superhorizon for more than ≈1 core e-fold are essentially erased: near-total suppression for k\_b e^{−2.5} ≲ k ≲ k\_b e^{−0.5}, full power for k \> k\_b | ZS-U12 §9.3 (Ch 107\) |
| Running n\_supp (integrated background) ✦ | The Einstein-frame rolldown integration confirms H\_b \= √(V\_E(0)/3) \= **A**/√6 exactly and shows H running down ≈1.8× over the core, so n\_supp rises for k \< k\_b — a running, not a fixed slope | ZS-U12 §9.4 (Ch 107\) |
| NC-U12.7 structural boundary ✦ | n\_supp \= √(12/λ\_vac)(−ln|**λ**|²) is irreducibly tied to λ\_vac \= 2**A**² (ZS-U5, DERIVED-CONDITIONAL) and to the OPEN uphill transition (ZS-A6 NC-A6.3); unconditional DERIVED is unreachable from this paper | ZS-U12 NC-U12.7 (Ch 107\) |
| Gate F-U12.8 ✦ | The conditional lift: λ\_vac \= 2**A**² PROVEN by ZS-U5 RG closure **and** ZS-A6 F-A6.1 NR confirmation of the near-de Sitter core would raise n\_supp ≈ 7.02 to DERIVED; otherwise the FORM survives and the exponent is recomputed | ZS-U12 Table 5 (Ch 107\) |
| Sharpness as the discriminant ✦ | The decisive CMB observable is not C₂ (cosmic-variance-limited) but the joint ℓ \= 2–10 TT+TE+EE cutoff sharpness against the ΛCDM ensemble; a broad ℓ ≈ 2–30 tilt falsifies n\_supp ≈ 7 (F-U12.4) | ZS-U12 §10.3, §12 (Ch 107\) |

**Version History**

v3.2 (May 2026): PART X — The Spin-Pair Coexistence Principle added between PART IX and the Master Bypass Index. Four DERIVED-interpretation readings consolidated:
